तद्रूपैःtadrūpaiḥwith the forms of those (gods)n. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument of manner
चण्डिकाम्caṇḍikāmto Caṇḍikāf. acc. sg. of चण्डिका; goal of ययुः
ययुःyayuḥwent√या (adādi 2P ‘to go’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • ब्रह्मेश brahmeśa → ब्रह्म + ईश
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    a + ī → e. The stem ब्रह्मन् drops its n at the head of a compound.
  • तथेन्द्रस्य tathendrasya → तथा + इन्द्रस्य
    vowel sandhi (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    ā + i → e.
  • शरीरेभ्यो विनिष्क्रम्य śarīrebhyo viniṣkramya → शरीरेभ्यः + विनिष्क्रम्य
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    -aḥ before the voiced v becomes -o.
  • तद्रूपैश्चण्डिकां tadrūpaiścaṇḍikāṃ → तद्रूपैः + चण्डिकाम्
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)
    Visarga before c becomes ś. Inside the compound, तद् + रूप keeps d before the voiced r (झलां जशोऽन्ते, 8.2.39).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • ब्रह्मेशगुहविष्णूनाम् brahmeśaguhaviṣṇūnām — itaretara dvandva
    Members: ब्रह्मन् + ईश + गुह + विष्णु · Vigraha: ब्रह्मा च ईशः च गुहः च विष्णुः च, तेषाम् brahmā ca īśaḥ ca guhaḥ ca viṣṇuḥ ca, teṣām
    Literally “of Brahmā, Īśa, Guha and Viṣṇu”; in the verse: four of the five gods whose powers appear; Indra is added separately
  • तद्रूपैः tadrūpaiḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: तद् + रूप · Vigraha: तेषां रूपाणि, तैः teṣāṃ rūpāṇi, taiḥ
    Literally “with the forms of them”; in the verse: each in the likeness of her god (explained in 8.14)

C. Morphology

  • विनिष्क्रम्य viniṣkramya — Absolutive with two prefixes (वि, निस्): the s of निस् becomes ṣ after i, and -य replaces -त्वा because the verb is prefixed.
  • ययुः yayuḥ — Perfect third plural of √या: reduplication य-यु with the ending -उः; the root ā is lost before it.

D. Anvaya — prose order

(अतिवीर्यबलान्विताः) ब्रह्मेशगुहविष्णूनां तथा इन्द्रस्य च शक्तयः शरीरेभ्यः विनिष्क्रम्य तद्रूपैः चण्डिकां ययुः।

(ativīryabalānvitāḥ) brahmeśaguhaviṣṇūnāṃ tathā indrasya ca śaktayaḥ śarīrebhyaḥ viniṣkramya tadrūpaiḥ caṇḍikāṃ yayuḥ |

The subject promised in 8.12 (शक्तयः) with its genitives, an absolutive of motion out of the gods’ bodies, and the main verb ययुः with its goal चण्डिकाम्.

6Translation

Literal— the Śaktis of Brahmā, Īśa, Guha and Viṣṇu, and likewise of Indra, having come forth from their bodies, went to Caṇḍikā with their forms.

Readable— the Śaktis of Brahmā, Śiva, Skanda and Viṣṇu, and of Indra too, came forth from their bodies and went to Caṇḍikā, each in the form of her god.

Īśa and Guha are rendered by their familiar names, Śiva and Skanda; ‘each in the form of her god’ interprets तद्रूपैः in the light of 8.14.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe powers of five gods leave their bodies and join Caṇḍikā. As in chapter 2, where the Goddess took shape from the radiance of all the gods, the divine power leaves the gods and gathers in her.

Traditional interpretationThese are the Mothers (मातृगण, 8.39): Brahmāṇī, Māheśvarī, Kaumārī, Vaiṣṇavī, Vārāhī, Nārasiṃhī and Aindrī, introduced one by one in 8.15–21. Vārāhī and Nārasiṃhī are Viṣṇu’s further forms; the gods are five but the Mothers seven.

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāIn Śrīvidyā worship eight Mothers — Brāhmī, Māheśvarī, Kaumārī, Vaiṣṇavī, Vārāhī, Māhendrī, Cāmuṇḍā and Mahālakṣmī — are worshipped in the outer square (bhūpura) of the Śrīcakra. The list overlaps with this chapter’s Mothers but belongs to a separate liturgical tradition.

8.14Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

यस्य देवस्य यद्रूपं यथाभूषणवाहनम्।
तद्वदेव हि तच्छक्तिरसुरान् योद्धुमाययौ ॥ १४ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

यस्य | देवस्य | यत् | रूपम् | यथा | भूषणवाहनम्तद्वत् | एव | हि | तच्छक्तिः | असुरान् | योद्धुम् | आययौ

3IAST

As recitedyasya devasya yadrūpaṃ yathābhūṣaṇavāhanam |
tadvadeva hi tacchaktirasurān yoddhumāyayau ||

Separatedyasya | devasya | yat | rūpam | yathā | bhūṣaṇavāhanam
tadvat | eva | hi | tacchaktiḥ | asurān | yoddhum | āyayau

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
यस्यyasyaof whicheverrelative pronoun यद्, m. gen. sg.; qualifies देवस्य
देवस्यdevasyagodm. gen. sg. of देव
यत्yatwhateverrelative pronoun यद्, n. nom. sg.; qualifies रूपम्
रूपम्rūpamformn. nom. sg. of रूप; subject of the relative clause (supply आसीत्)
यथाyathāas, in whatever wayindeclinable (relative adverb of manner)
भूषणवाहनम्bhūṣaṇavāhanamornaments and vehiclen. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
तद्वत्tadvatlike that, in the same wayindeclinable (तद् + vati, ‘like that’); correlative of यत् and यथा
एवevaexactly, justindeclinable (restrictive particle)
हिhiindeedindeclinable (emphatic particle)
तच्छक्तिःtacchaktiḥhis Śaktif. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of आययौ
असुरान्asurānthe asurasm. acc. pl. of असुर; object of योद्धुम्
योद्धुम्yoddhumto fightinfinitive (tumun) of √युध् (divādi 4Ā ‘to fight’)
आययौāyayaucameआ + √या (adādi 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • यद्रूपं yadrūpaṃ → यत् + रूपम्
    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)
    Final t becomes d before the voiced r; the m of रूपम् becomes anusvāra before य (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).
  • तद्वदेव tadvadeva → तद्वत् + एव
    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)
    Final t becomes d before a vowel.
  • तच्छक्तिरसुरान् tacchaktirasurān → तच्छक्तिः + असुरान्
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    -iḥ before a vowel → -ir. Inside the compound, तद् + शक्ति → तच्छक्ति: t becomes c next to ś (स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः, 8.4.40), and ś after the stop becomes ch (शश्छोऽटि, 8.4.63).
  • योद्धुमाययौ yoddhumāyayau → योद्धुम् + आययौ
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    The m is kept and written together with the vowel.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • भूषणवाहनम् bhūṣaṇavāhanam — samāhāra dvandva
    Members: भूषण + वाहन · Vigraha: भूषणं च वाहनं च, तयोः समाहारः bhūṣaṇaṃ ca vāhanaṃ ca, tayoḥ samāhāraḥ
    Literally “ornament-and-vehicle”; in the verse: his insignia and his mount
    Alternative: With the preceding यथा the whole may be read as one avyayībhāva, यथाभूषणवाहनम् ‘according to (his) ornaments and vehicle’; the meaning does not change. यथा is taken as a separate relative adverb because it pairs with तद्वत्.
  • तच्छक्तिः tacchaktiḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: तद् + शक्ति · Vigraha: तस्य शक्तिः tasya śaktiḥ
    Literally “the power of him”; in the verse: that god’s own Śakti

C. Morphology

  • तद्वत् tadvat — The taddhita -वत् (vati) makes an adverb of likeness: तद्वत् ‘like that’. It answers the relatives यत् and यथा.
  • योद्धुम् yoddhum — Infinitive of purpose with a verb of motion: ‘came to fight’. √युध् + -तुम्: dh + t → ddh.
  • आययौ āyayau — Perfect third singular of आ + √या: reduplication य-यौ.

D. Anvaya — prose order

यस्य देवस्य यत् रूपम्, यथा (च) भूषणवाहनम्, तद्वत् एव हि तच्छक्तिः असुरान् योद्धुम् आययौ।

yasya devasya yat rūpam, yathā (ca) bhūṣaṇavāhanam, tadvat eva hi tacchaktiḥ asurān yoddhum āyayau |

A relative–correlative sentence: यत् … यथा … are answered by तद्वत् एव; the main clause is तच्छक्तिः … आययौ.

6Translation

LiteralOf whichever god whatever was the form, and as (were his) ornaments and vehicle, just so indeed his Śakti came to fight the asuras.

ReadableWhatever form each god had, whatever his ornaments and his mount, in just that form his Śakti came to fight the asuras.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe rule for all that follows: each Śakti has her god’s form, insignia and mount. Verses 8.15–21 apply it one by one.

Traditional interpretationIn Śākta thought the god acts only through his Śakti. Here the Śaktis come without their gods and fight in their likeness — the power, not its bearer, takes the field.

8.15Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

हंसयुक्तविमानाग्रे साक्षसूत्रकमण्डलुः।
आयाता ब्रह्मणः शक्तिर्ब्रह्माणी साभिधीयते ॥ १५ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

हंसयुक्तविमानाग्रे | साक्षसूत्रकमण्डलुःआयाता | ब्रह्मणः | शक्तिः | ब्रह्माणी | सा | अभिधीयते

3IAST

As recitedhaṃsayuktavimānāgre sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ |
āyātā brahmaṇaḥ śaktirbrahmāṇī sābhidhīyate ||

Separatedhaṃsayuktavimānāgre | sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ
āyātā | brahmaṇaḥ | śaktiḥ | brahmāṇī | sā | abhidhīyate

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
हंसयुक्तविमानाग्रेhaṃsayuktavimānāgreat the front of a celestial car yoked with swansn. loc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
साक्षसूत्रकमण्डलुःsākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥwith rosary and water-potf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies शक्तिः
आयाताāyātācame, arrivedpast participle (kta) of आ + √या, f. nom. sg.; used as the finite verb
ब्रह्मणःbrahmaṇaḥof Brahmām. gen. sg. of ब्रह्मन् (n-stem); depends on शक्तिः
शक्तिःśaktiḥthe Śaktif. nom. sg. of शक्ति; subject of आयाता
ब्रह्माणीbrahmāṇīBrahmāṇīf. nom. sg. of ब्रह्माणी; predicate with अभिधीयते
साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of अभिधीयते
अभिधीयतेabhidhīyateis calledअभि + √धा (juhotyādi 3 ‘to place’; with अभि ‘to name’), karmaṇi prayoga, laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • विमानाग्रे vimānāgre → विमान + अग्रे
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā; so too स + अक्ष- → साक्ष-.
  • शक्तिर्ब्रह्माणी śaktirbrahmāṇī → शक्तिः + ब्रह्माणी
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    -iḥ before the voiced b → -ir.
  • साभिधीयते sābhidhīyate → सा + अभिधीयते
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • हंसयुक्तविमानाग्रे haṃsayuktavimānāgre — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, containing a karmadhāraya and a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (हंसयुक्त)
    Members: हंसयुक्त + विमान + अग्र · Vigraha: हंसैः युक्तं हंसयुक्तम्; हंसयुक्तं च तत् विमानं च हंसयुक्तविमानम्; तस्य अग्रम्, तस्मिन् haṃsaiḥ yuktaṃ haṃsayuktam; haṃsayuktaṃ ca tat vimānaṃ ca haṃsayuktavimānam; tasya agram, tasmin
    Literally “at the front of the swan-yoked car”; in the verse: riding in Brahmā’s swan-drawn chariot
  • साक्षसूत्रकमण्डलुः sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ — bahuvrīhi (saha-bahuvrīhi) on an itaretara dvandva
    Members: स + अक्षसूत्र + कमण्डलु · Vigraha: अक्षसूत्रेण कमण्डलुना च सह वर्तते या सा (अक्षसूत्रम् = अक्षाणां सूत्रम्) akṣasūtreṇa kamaṇḍalunā ca saha vartate yā sā (akṣasūtram = akṣāṇāṃ sūtram)
    Literally “she who is with rosary and water-pot”; in the verse: bearing Brahmā’s rosary and ascetic’s water-pot

C. Morphology

  • ब्रह्माणी brahmāṇī — Feminine of ब्रह्मन् with the augment -आन- and -ई (as इन्द्राणी from इन्द्र, रुद्राणी from रुद्र): ‘the consort or power of Brahmā’.
  • अभिधीयते abhidhīyate — Passive present of अभि + √धा: the root vowel becomes ī before the passive -य- (धीयते). ‘Is named’, with the name in the nominative.
  • साक्षसूत्रकमण्डलुः sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ — A bahuvrīhi ending in a u-stem keeps that stem in the feminine: nominative -उः, not -ऊ.

D. Anvaya — prose order

हंसयुक्तविमानाग्रे साक्षसूत्रकमण्डलुः ब्रह्मणः शक्तिः आयाता; सा ब्रह्माणी (इति) अभिधीयते।

haṃsayuktavimānāgre sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ brahmaṇaḥ śaktiḥ āyātā; sā brahmāṇī (iti) abhidhīyate |

Two clauses: the arrival (शक्तिः आयाता, with place and attributes), then the name (सा ब्रह्माणी अभिधीयते).

6Translation

LiteralAt the front of a car yoked with swans, with rosary and water-pot, came the Śakti of Brahmā; she is called Brahmāṇī.

ReadableBrahmā’s Śakti came seated in a chariot drawn by swans, bearing a rosary and a water-pot. She is called Brahmāṇī.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe first of the Mothers mirrors Brahmā: the swan-drawn car, the rosary and the water-pot. Her water-pot becomes a weapon when the battle begins (8.33).

Traditional interpretationThe gods praise her in this form in chapter 11 (11.13: हंसयुक्तविमानस्थे ब्रह्माणीरूपधारिणि), and at 10.6 the Mothers, ‘with Brahmāṇī at their head’ (ब्रह्माणीप्रमुखाः), return into the one Goddess.

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāThe Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) names the Goddess ब्राह्मी (name 675), the other form of the same feminine of Brahmā. The two texts use different forms; the identity is of idea, not of wording.

8.16Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

माहेश्वरी वृषारूढा त्रिशूलवरधारिणी।
महाहिवलया प्राप्ता चन्द्ररेखाविभूषणा ॥ १६ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

माहेश्वरी | वृषारूढा | त्रिशूलवरधारिणीमहाहिवलया | प्राप्ता | चन्द्ररेखाविभूषणा

3IAST

As recitedmāheśvarī vṛṣārūḍhā triśūlavaradhāriṇī |
mahāhivalayā prāptā candrarekhāvibhūṣaṇā ||

Separatedmāheśvarī | vṛṣārūḍhā | triśūlavaradhāriṇī
mahāhivalayā | prāptā | candrarekhāvibhūṣaṇā

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
माहेश्वरीmāheśvarīMāheśvarī, the Śakti of Maheśvara (Śiva)f. nom. sg. of माहेश्वरी (taddhita: महेश्वर + vṛddhi + ṅīp, ‘belonging to Maheśvara’); subject of प्राप्ता
वृषारूढाvṛṣārūḍhāmounted on the bullf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies माहेश्वरी
त्रिशूलवरधारिणीtriśūlavaradhāriṇībearing a mighty tridentf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies माहेश्वरी
महाहिवलयाmahāhivalayāwith great serpents for braceletsf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies माहेश्वरी
प्राप्ताprāptāarrivedpast participle (kta) of प्र + √आप् (svādi 5P ‘to reach’), f. nom. sg.; used as the finite verb
चन्द्ररेखाविभूषणाcandrarekhāvibhūṣaṇāadorned with the crescent moonf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies माहेश्वरी

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • वृषारूढा vṛṣārūḍhā → वृष + आरूढा
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā.
  • महाहिवलया mahāhivalayā → महा + अहि + वलया
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    महत् takes the form महा at the head of a karmadhāraya; ā + a → ā. The verse has no external sandhi: every word ends in a vowel before a consonant.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • वृषारूढा vṛṣārūḍhā — dvitīyā tatpuruṣa
    Members: वृष + आरूढ · Vigraha: वृषम् आरूढा vṛṣam ārūḍhā
    Literally “having mounted the bull”; in the verse: riding Śiva’s bull
  • त्रिशूलवरधारिणी triśūlavaradhāriṇī — upapada tatpuruṣa (धारिन्, ‘holding’, as final member), containing a karmadhāraya (त्रिशूलवर, वर ‘excellent’ standing second) and a bahuvrīhi (त्रिशूल)
    Members: त्रिशूलवर + धारिन् · Vigraha: त्रिशूलवरं धारयति इति (त्रिशूलवरम् = वरं त्रिशूलम्; त्रिशूलम् = त्रीणि शूलानि यस्मिन् तत्) triśūlavaraṃ dhārayati iti (triśūlavaram = varaṃ triśūlam; triśūlam = trīṇi śūlāni yasmin tat)
    Literally “she who holds an excellent trident”; in the verse: bearing Śiva’s trident
  • महाहिवलया mahāhivalayā — bahuvrīhi containing a karmadhāraya (महाहि)
    Members: महाहि + वलय · Vigraha: महान्तः अहयः वलयानि यस्याः सा (महाहिः = महान् चासौ अहिः च) mahāntaḥ ahayaḥ valayāni yasyāḥ sā (mahāhiḥ = mahān cāsau ahiḥ ca)
    Literally “she whose bracelets are great serpents”; in the verse: wearing serpents as Śiva does
  • चन्द्ररेखाविभूषणा candrarekhāvibhūṣaṇā — bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: चन्द्ररेखा + विभूषण · Vigraha: चन्द्ररेखा विभूषणं यस्याः सा (चन्द्ररेखा = चन्द्रस्य रेखा) candrarekhā vibhūṣaṇaṃ yasyāḥ sā (candrarekhā = candrasya rekhā)
    Literally “she whose ornament is the line of the moon”; in the verse: crowned with the crescent

C. Morphology

  • माहेश्वरी māheśvarī — Vṛddhi of the first syllable (म → मा) forms the adjective ‘of Maheśvara’; the feminine ई makes it the name of his Śakti. Vaiṣṇavī, Kaumārī and Nārasiṃhī are formed the same way.
  • प्राप्ता prāptā — A past participle standing for a finite verb (‘arrived’), the usual way these verses mark each arrival (8.20, 8.21).

D. Anvaya — prose order

वृषारूढा त्रिशूलवरधारिणी महाहिवलया चन्द्ररेखाविभूषणा माहेश्वरी प्राप्ता।

vṛṣārūḍhā triśūlavaradhāriṇī mahāhivalayā candrarekhāvibhūṣaṇā māheśvarī prāptā |

One subject (माहेश्वरी), four qualifying compounds, one verb (प्राप्ता).

6Translation

LiteralMāheśvarī, mounted on the bull, holding an excellent trident, with great serpents as bracelets, adorned with the crescent of the moon, arrived.

ReadableMāheśvarī came, riding the bull and bearing a mighty trident, with great serpents for her bracelets and the crescent moon for her ornament.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningŚiva’s Śakti carries every mark of Śiva: the bull, the trident, the serpents and the crescent moon.

Traditional interpretationThe gods praise her in the same form at 11.14 (त्रिशूलचन्द्राहिधरे महावृषभवाहिनि).

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāThe Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has the name माहेश्वरी (name 208). There it names the supreme Goddess herself, not one Mother among seven.

8.17Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

कौमारी शक्तिहस्ता च मयूरवरवाहना।
योद्धुमभ्याययौ दैत्यानम्बिका गुहरूपिणी ॥ १७ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

कौमारी | शक्तिहस्ता | च | मयूरवरवाहनायोद्धुम् | अभ्याययौ | दैत्यान् | अम्बिका | गुहरूपिणी

3IAST

As recitedkaumārī śaktihastā ca mayūravaravāhanā |
yoddhumabhyāyayau daityānambikā guharūpiṇī ||

Separatedkaumārī | śaktihastā | ca | mayūravaravāhanā
yoddhum | abhyāyayau | daityān | ambikā | guharūpiṇī

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
कौमारीkaumārīKaumārī, the Śakti of Kumāra (Skanda)f. nom. sg. of कौमारी (taddhita from कुमार); subject of अभ्याययौ
शक्तिहस्ताśaktihastāspear in handf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies कौमारी
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
मयूरवरवाहनाmayūravaravāhanāriding a splendid peacockf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies कौमारी
योद्धुम्yoddhumto fightinfinitive (tumun) of √युध् (divādi 4Ā)
अभ्याययौabhyāyayaucame forward, advancedअभि + आ + √या (adādi 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
दैत्यान्daityānthe Daityasm. acc. pl. of दैत्य; object of योद्धुम्
अम्बिकाambikāthe Motherf. nom. sg. of अम्बिका; in apposition to कौमारी
गुहरूपिणीguharūpiṇīin the form of Guha (Skanda)f. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अम्बिका

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • योद्धुमभ्याययौ yoddhumabhyāyayau → योद्धुम् + अभ्याययौ
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    Inside the verb, अभि + आ- → अभ्या- (इको यणचि, 6.1.77).
  • दैत्यानम्बिका daityānambikā → दैत्यान् + अम्बिका
    no change (final n before a vowel) · ङमो ह्रस्वादचि ङमुण्नित्यम् (8.3.32) does not apply after a long vowel
    The n is doubled only after a short vowel (as in एतस्मिन्नन्तरे, 8.12); after ā it is simply joined.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • शक्तिहस्ता śaktihastā — bahuvrīhi (vyadhikaraṇa: the members in different cases)
    Members: शक्ति + हस्त · Vigraha: शक्तिः हस्ते यस्याः सा śaktiḥ haste yasyāḥ sā
    Literally “she in whose hand is the spear”; in the verse: holding Skanda’s spear
  • मयूरवरवाहना mayūravaravāhanā — bahuvrīhi containing a karmadhāraya (मयूरवर, with वर ‘excellent’ standing second)
    Members: मयूरवर + वाहन · Vigraha: मयूरवरः वाहनं यस्याः सा (मयूरवरः = वरः मयूरः) mayūravaraḥ vāhanaṃ yasyāḥ sā (mayūravaraḥ = varaḥ mayūraḥ)
    Literally “she whose vehicle is an excellent peacock”; in the verse: riding Skanda’s peacock
  • गुहरूपिणी guharūpiṇī — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa with the possessive taddhita -इन् (feminine -इनी)
    Members: गुहरूप + इन् · Vigraha: गुहस्य रूपं गुहरूपम्; तत् अस्याः अस्ति इति गुहरूपिणी guhasya rūpaṃ guharūpam; tat asyāḥ asti iti guharūpiṇī
    Literally “possessing Guha’s form”; in the verse: Skanda’s power in his likeness

C. Morphology

  • शक्तिहस्ता śaktihastā — शक्ति here is Skanda’s weapon, the spear; the same word names the goddess as a ‘power’ (शक्तिः, 8.13). The verse plays on both.
  • अभ्याययौ abhyāyayau — Perfect of √या with two prefixes, अभि and आ: ‘came towards’ (the enemy).

D. Anvaya — prose order

शक्तिहस्ता मयूरवरवाहना च गुहरूपिणी कौमारी अम्बिका दैत्यान् योद्धुम् अभ्याययौ।

śaktihastā mayūravaravāhanā ca guharūpiṇī kaumārī ambikā daityān yoddhum abhyāyayau |

Subject (कौमारी, with अम्बिका in apposition and three qualifiers), infinitive of purpose with its object, verb.

6Translation

LiteralKaumārī, spear in hand and with a splendid peacock as her vehicle, the Mother in the form of Guha, came forward to fight the Daityas.

ReadableKaumārī, spear in hand and riding a splendid peacock, came forward to fight the Daityas — the Mother in the form of Guha.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningSkanda’s Śakti has his spear and his peacock. She is called अम्बिका, ‘Mother’, the name the text otherwise gives to the Goddess herself: each Mother is the one Mother in another form.

Traditional interpretationThe gods praise her at 11.15 as attended by peacock and cock and bearing the great spear (मयूरकुक्कुटवृते महाशक्तिधरे).

8.18Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

तथैव वैष्णवी शक्तिर्गरुडोपरि संस्थिता।
शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ताभ्युपाययौ ॥ १८ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

तथा | एव | वैष्णवी | शक्तिः | गरुडोपरि | संस्थिताशङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ता | अभ्युपाययौ

3IAST

As recitedtathaiva vaiṣṇavī śaktirgaruḍopari saṃsthitā |
śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastābhyupāyayau ||

Separatedtathā | eva | vaiṣṇavī | śaktiḥ | garuḍopari | saṃsthitā
śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastā | abhyupāyayau

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
तथाtathāso, likewiseindeclinable (adverb of manner)
एवevajust, in the same wayindeclinable (restrictive particle)
वैष्णवीvaiṣṇavīof Viṣṇu, Vaiṣṇavīf. nom. sg. of वैष्णवी (taddhita from विष्णु); qualifies शक्तिः
शक्तिःśaktiḥthe Śaktif. nom. sg. of शक्ति; subject of अभ्युपाययौ
गरुडोपरिgaruḍopariupon Garuḍaindeclinable compound (see Samāsa), locative sense
संस्थिताsaṃsthitāseatedpast participle (kta) of सम् + √स्था (bhvādi 1P ‘to stand’), f. nom. sg.
शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ताśaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastāwith conch, discus, mace, the bow Śārṅga and sword in her handsf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies शक्तिः
अभ्युपाययौabhyupāyayaucame up, drew nearअभि + उप + आ + √या (adādi 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • तथैव tathaiva → तथा + एव
    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    ā + e → ai.
  • शक्तिर्गरुडोपरि śaktirgaruḍopari → शक्तिः + गरुडोपरि
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    -iḥ before the voiced g → -ir. Inside the compound, गरुड + उपरि → गरुडोपरि (a + u → o, आद्गुणः, 6.1.87).
  • हस्ताभ्युपाययौ hastābhyupāyayau → हस्ता + अभ्युपाययौ
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + a → ā. Inside the verb अभि + उप- → अभ्युप- (इको यणचि, 6.1.77).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • गरुडोपरि garuḍopari — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa with the indeclinable उपरि as final member
    Members: गरुड + उपरि · Vigraha: गरुडस्य उपरि garuḍasya upari
    Literally “on top of Garuḍa”; in the verse: riding Viṣṇu’s eagle
    Alternative: Classical prose keeps the genitive apart (सिंहस्योपरि, 7.3); epic verse often compounds such phrases. The meaning is the same.
  • शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ता śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastā — bahuvrīhi on an itaretara dvandva
    Members: शङ्ख + चक्र + गदा + शार्ङ्ग + खड्ग + हस्त · Vigraha: शङ्खः च चक्रं च गदा च शार्ङ्गं च खड्गः च शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गाः; ते हस्तेषु यस्याः सा śaṅkhaḥ ca cakraṃ ca gadā ca śārṅgaṃ ca khaḍgaḥ ca śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgāḥ; te hasteṣu yasyāḥ sā
    Literally “she in whose hands are conch, discus, mace, Śārṅga and sword”; in the verse: bearing all Viṣṇu’s weapons

C. Morphology

  • अभ्युपाययौ abhyupāyayau — Three prefixes on √या (अभि, उप, आ): ‘came up close’. The perfect stem is ययौ, as in आययौ (8.14).
  • वैष्णवी vaiṣṇavī — Vṛddhi derivative of विष्णु (वि → वै, u → av): ‘belonging to Viṣṇu’, with the feminine ई.

D. Anvaya — prose order

तथा एव गरुडोपरि संस्थिता शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ता वैष्णवी शक्तिः अभ्युपाययौ।

tathā eva garuḍopari saṃsthitā śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastā vaiṣṇavī śaktiḥ abhyupāyayau |

Subject (वैष्णवी शक्तिः) with two qualifiers; one verb.

6Translation

LiteralJust so the Vaiṣṇavī Śakti, seated upon Garuḍa, with conch, discus, mace, Śārṅga and sword in her hands, came up.

ReadableIn the same way Viṣṇu’s Śakti, Vaiṣṇavī, came near, seated on Garuḍa, bearing conch, discus, mace, the bow Śārṅga and the sword.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningViṣṇu’s Śakti rides his eagle Garuḍa and bears his five weapons, Śārṅga being the name of his bow.

Traditional interpretationThe gods praise her at 11.16 with the same weapons (शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गगृहीतपरमायुधे). Viṣṇu sends two more Śaktis, Vārāhī and Nārasiṃhī, from his avatāra forms (8.19–20).

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāThe Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has the name वैष्णवी (name 892), there of the supreme Goddess. The two works are distinct.

8.19Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

यज्ञवाराहमतुलं रूपं या बिभ्रतो हरेः।
शक्तिः साप्याययौ तत्र वाराहीं बिभ्रती तनुम् ॥ १९ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

यज्ञवाराहम् | अतुलम् | रूपम् | या | बिभ्रतः | हरेःशक्तिः | सा | अपि | आययौ | तत्र | वाराहीम् | बिभ्रती | तनुम्

3IAST

As recitedyajñavārāhamatulaṃ rūpaṃ yā bibhrato hareḥ |
śaktiḥ sāpyāyayau tatra vārāhīṃ bibhratī tanum ||

Separatedyajñavārāham | atulam | rūpam | yā | bibhrataḥ | hareḥ
śaktiḥ | sā | api | āyayau | tatra | vārāhīm | bibhratī | tanum

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
यज्ञवाराहम्yajñavārāhamof the Sacrificial Boarn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies रूपम्
अतुलम्atulamincomparablen. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies रूपम्
रूपम्rūpamformn. acc. sg. of रूप; object of बिभ्रतः
याyāwho, she whorelative pronoun यद्, f. nom. sg.; answered by सा
बिभ्रतःbibhrataḥbearing, who bearspresent participle (śatṛ) of √भृ (juhotyādi 3P ‘to bear’), m. gen. sg.; qualifies हरेः
हरेःhareḥof Hari (Viṣṇu)m. gen. sg. of हरि (i-stem); depends on शक्तिः
शक्तिःśaktiḥthe Śaktif. nom. sg. of शक्ति; predicate of the relative clause (या … शक्तिः)
साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; correlative of या; subject of आययौ
अपिapialso, tooindeclinable (particle)
आययौāyayaucameआ + √या (adādi 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place, तद् + tral)
वाराहीम्vārāhīmboar-shaped, of a boarf. acc. sg. of the adjective वाराह (feminine वाराही); qualifies तनुम्
बिभ्रतीbibhratībearing, takingpresent participle (śatṛ) of √भृ, f. nom. sg.; qualifies सा
तनुम्tanumbodyf. acc. sg. of तनु (u-stem); object of बिभ्रती

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • यज्ञवाराहमतुलं रूपं या yajñavārāhamatulaṃ rūpaṃ yā → यज्ञवाराहम् + अतुलम् + रूपम् + या
    final m before a vowel; anusvāra · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    m stays before the vowel of अतुलम्, and becomes anusvāra before r and y.
  • बिभ्रतो हरेः bibhrato hareḥ → बिभ्रतः + हरेः
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    -aḥ before the voiced h becomes -o.
  • साप्याययौ sāpyāyayau → सा + अपि + आययौ
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha and yaṇ) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    ā + a → ā (साप-); i before ā becomes y (-प्याययौ).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • यज्ञवाराहम् yajñavārāham — karmadhāraya (madhyamapadalopī: the middle word मय ‘made of’ understood)
    Members: यज्ञ + वाराह · Vigraha: यज्ञमयं वाराहम् (रूपम्) yajñamayaṃ vārāham (rūpam)
    Literally “the sacrifice-boar (form)”; in the verse: Viṣṇu’s Boar avatāra, whose body is the sacrifice
    Alternative: Or a taddhita on the compound यज्ञवराह ‘the Sacrifice-Boar’: यज्ञवराहस्य इदम् ‘belonging to the Sacrificial Boar’. Either way it describes the form Hari bears.
  • अतुलम् atulam — bahuvrīhi (nañ-bahuvrīhi)
    Members: अ + तुला · Vigraha: न विद्यते तुला यस्य तत् na vidyate tulā yasya tat
    Literally “that which has no equal”; in the verse: matchless

C. Morphology

  • बिभ्रतः bibhrataḥ — Present participle of √भृ (third class, reduplicated बिभर्-/बिभृ-). Reduplicating verbs have no nasal in the participle: बिभ्रत्, m. gen. sg. बिभ्रतः, f. बिभ्रती (next line).
  • वाराहीम् vārāhīm — Feminine of the vṛddhi adjective वाराह ‘of a boar’ (from वराह); with तनुम् ‘a boar’s body’. It is also the name the Śakti bears (Vārāhī, 8.49).
  • आययौ āyayau — Perfect of आ + √या, as in 8.14.

D. Anvaya — prose order

या अतुलं यज्ञवाराहं रूपं बिभ्रतः हरेः शक्तिः, सा अपि वाराहीं तनुं बिभ्रती तत्र आययौ।

yā atulaṃ yajñavārāhaṃ rūpaṃ bibhrataḥ hareḥ śaktiḥ, sā api vārāhīṃ tanuṃ bibhratī tatra āyayau |

A relative clause (या … हरेः शक्तिः) identifies the subject; the main clause (सा अपि … आययौ) has the participle phrase वाराहीं तनुं बिभ्रती.

6Translation

LiteralShe who was the Śakti of Hari, who bears the incomparable form of the Sacrificial Boar — she too came there, bearing a boar’s body.

ReadableThe Śakti of Hari — of him who takes the matchless form of the Sacrificial Boar — came there too, in the body of a boar.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningA second Śakti of Viṣṇu, from his Boar avatāra. The principle of 8.14 holds: as the god appears, so his Śakti.

Traditional interpretationIn the Purāṇas the Boar who lifts the earth from the waters is the sacrifice embodied (yajña-varāha). The gods praise Vārāhī at 11.17 as the one who ‘raised up the earth with her tusk’ (दंष्ट्रोद्धृतवसुंधरे).

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāVārāhī is also a warrior power in the Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa): name 76, विशुक्रप्राणहरणवाराहीवीर्यनन्दिता, ‘delighted by the valour of Vārāhī in taking Viśukra’s life’. The two battles are separate stories.

8.20Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

नारसिंही नृसिंहस्य बिभ्रती सदृशं वपुः।
प्राप्ता तत्र सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिः ॥ २० ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

नारसिंही | नृसिंहस्य | बिभ्रती | सदृशम् | वपुःप्राप्ता | तत्र | सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिः

3IAST

As recitednārasiṃhī nṛsiṃhasya bibhratī sadṛśaṃ vapuḥ |
prāptā tatra saṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥ ||

Separatednārasiṃhī | nṛsiṃhasya | bibhratī | sadṛśam | vapuḥ
prāptā | tatra | saṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
नारसिंहीnārasiṃhīNārasiṃhī, the Śakti of the Man-Lionf. nom. sg. of नारसिंही (taddhita from नरसिंह); subject of प्राप्ता
नृसिंहस्यnṛsiṃhasyaof Nṛsiṃha, the Man-Lionm. gen. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); construed with सदृशम्
बिभ्रतीbibhratībearingpresent participle (śatṛ) of √भृ (juhotyādi 3P), f. nom. sg.; qualifies नारसिंही
सदृशम्sadṛśamlike, resemblingn. acc. sg. of सदृश; qualifies वपुः; takes the genitive नृसिंहस्य
वपुःvapuḥbody, formn. acc. sg. of वपुस् (s-stem); object of बिभ्रती
प्राप्ताprāptāarrivedpast participle (kta) of प्र + √आप्, f. nom. sg.; used as the finite verb
तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place)
सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिःsaṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥshe by the toss of whose mane the hosts of stars were scatteredf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies नारसिंही

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • सदृशं वपुः sadṛśaṃ vapuḥ → सदृशम् + वपुः
    anusvāra · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra.
  • सटाक्षेप saṭākṣepa → सटा + आक्षेप
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + ā → ā.
  • वपुः प्राप्ता vapuḥ prāptā → वपुः + प्राप्ता
    visarga sandhi (no change) · खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)
    Before an unvoiced p the visarga stays.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • नृसिंहस्य nṛsiṃhasya — karmadhāraya
    Members: नृ + सिंह · Vigraha: ना चासौ सिंहः च, तस्य nā cāsau siṃhaḥ ca, tasya
    Literally “of the man who is a lion”; in the verse: of Viṣṇu’s Man-Lion avatāra
    Alternative: Also analysed as a madhyamapadalopī karmadhāraya, नराकारः सिंहः ‘a lion in a man’s shape’. The name is the same.
  • सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिः saṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥ — bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (सटाक्षेप), a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (सटाक्षेपक्षिप्त) and a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (नक्षत्रसंहति)
    Members: सटा + आक्षेप + क्षिप्त + नक्षत्र + संहति · Vigraha: सटानाम् आक्षेपः सटाक्षेपः; तेन क्षिप्ता नक्षत्राणां संहतिः यया सा saṭānām ākṣepaḥ saṭākṣepaḥ; tena kṣiptā nakṣatrāṇāṃ saṃhatiḥ yayā sā
    Literally “she by whom the multitude of stars was scattered by the tossing of the mane”; in the verse: her shaking mane flung the stars about

C. Morphology

  • बिभ्रती bibhratī — Feminine of the present participle बिभ्रत् (√भृ), without a nasal, as with all reduplicating verbs.
  • वपुः vapuḥ — Neuter s-stem: nominative and accusative singular वपुः.
  • नारसिंही nārasiṃhī — Vṛddhi derivative of नरसिंह (न → ना) with the feminine ई: ‘belonging to the Man-Lion’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

नृसिंहस्य सदृशं वपुः बिभ्रती सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिः नारसिंही तत्र प्राप्ता।

nṛsiṃhasya sadṛśaṃ vapuḥ bibhratī saṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥ nārasiṃhī tatra prāptā |

Subject (नारसिंही) with a participle phrase and a bahuvrīhi; verb प्राप्ता.

6Translation

LiteralNārasiṃhī, bearing a body like that of Nṛsiṃha, arrived there — she by the tossing of whose mane the multitude of stars was scattered.

ReadableNārasiṃhī came there in a body like the Man-Lion’s, the tossing of her mane scattering the hosts of stars.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe third Śakti from Viṣṇu, the power of the Man-Lion. The image of the mane scattering the stars gives her cosmic size.

Traditional interpretationThe gods praise her at 11.18 as the one who, in the fierce Man-Lion form, set out to slay the Daityas (नृसिंहरूपेणोग्रेण हन्तुं दैत्यान् कृतोद्यमे). In the battle she tears the asuras with her claws (8.37).

8.21Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

वज्रहस्ता तथैवैन्द्री गजराजोपरि स्थिता।
प्राप्ता सहस्रनयना यथा शक्रस्तथैव सा ॥ २१ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

वज्रहस्ता | तथा | एव | ऐन्द्री | गजराजोपरि | स्थिताप्राप्ता | सहस्रनयना | यथा | शक्रः | तथा | एव | सा

3IAST

As recitedvajrahastā tathaivaindrī gajarājopari sthitā |
prāptā sahasranayanā yathā śakrastathaiva sā ||

Separatedvajrahastā | tathā | eva | aindrī | gajarājopari | sthitā
prāptā | sahasranayanā | yathā | śakraḥ | tathā | eva | sā

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
वज्रहस्ताvajrahastāthunderbolt in handf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies ऐन्द्री
तथाtathāso, likewiseindeclinable (adverb of manner)
एवevajust, in the same wayindeclinable (restrictive particle)
ऐन्द्रीaindrīAindrī, the Śakti of Indraf. nom. sg. of ऐन्द्री (taddhita from इन्द्र); subject of प्राप्ता
गजराजोपरिgajarājopariupon the king of elephantsindeclinable compound (see Samāsa), locative sense
स्थिताsthitāstanding, mountedpast participle (kta) of √स्था (bhvādi 1P), f. nom. sg.
प्राप्ताprāptāarrivedpast participle (kta) of प्र + √आप्, f. nom. sg.; used as the finite verb
सहस्रनयनाsahasranayanāthousand-eyedf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies ऐन्द्री
यथाyathāasindeclinable (relative adverb of manner)
शक्रःśakraḥŚakra (Indra)m. nom. sg. of शक्र; subject of the comparison (supply अस्ति)
तथाtathāsoindeclinable (correlative of यथा)
एवevaexactlyindeclinable (restrictive particle)
साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of the comparison

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • तथैवैन्द्री tathaivaindrī → तथा + एव + ऐन्द्री
    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    ā + e → ai (तथैव); then a + ai → ai (-वैन्द्री).
  • गजराजोपरि gajarājopari → गजराज + उपरि
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    a + u → o.
  • शक्रस्तथैव śakrastathaiva → शक्रः + तथा + एव
    visarga sandhi and vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    Visarga before t becomes s; ā + e → ai.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • वज्रहस्ता vajrahastā — bahuvrīhi (vyadhikaraṇa)
    Members: वज्र + हस्त · Vigraha: वज्रं हस्ते यस्याः सा vajraṃ haste yasyāḥ sā
    Literally “she in whose hand is the thunderbolt”; in the verse: bearing Indra’s weapon
  • गजराजोपरि gajarājopari — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa with the indeclinable उपरि as final member, containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (गजराज)
    Members: गजराज + उपरि · Vigraha: गजराजस्य उपरि (गजराजः = गजानां राजा) gajarājasya upari (gajarājaḥ = gajānāṃ rājā)
    Literally “on top of the king of elephants”; in the verse: riding Indra’s elephant Airāvata
    Alternative: As with गरुडोपरि (8.18), prose would keep the genitive separate.
  • सहस्रनयना sahasranayanā — bahuvrīhi
    Members: सहस्र + नयन · Vigraha: सहस्रं नयनानि यस्याः सा sahasraṃ nayanāni yasyāḥ sā
    Literally “she who has a thousand eyes”; in the verse: thousand-eyed like Indra

C. Morphology

  • गजराजोपरि gajarājopari — राजन् at the end of a tatpuruṣa becomes राज (a samāsānta suffix): गजानां राजा → गजराजः.
  • ऐन्द्री aindrī — Vṛddhi derivative of इन्द्र (इ → ऐ) with the feminine ई: ‘belonging to Indra’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

तथा एव वज्रहस्ता गजराजोपरि स्थिता सहस्रनयना ऐन्द्री प्राप्ता; यथा शक्रः, तथा एव सा।

tathā eva vajrahastā gajarājopari sthitā sahasranayanā aindrī prāptā; yathā śakraḥ, tathā eva sā |

A main clause (ऐन्द्री प्राप्ता with three qualifiers), then a verbless comparison यथा … तथा एव.

6Translation

LiteralJust so Aindrī, thunderbolt in hand, standing upon the king of elephants, arrived, thousand-eyed: as Śakra is, exactly so is she.

ReadableAindrī came likewise, thunderbolt in hand, mounted on the king of elephants and thousand-eyed — in every way as Indra is.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe last of the five gods’ Śaktis: Indra’s, with his thunderbolt, his elephant and his thousand eyes. The closing words restate the rule of 8.14: as the god, so his Śakti.

Traditional interpretationThe gods praise her at 11.19 as crowned, bearing the great thunderbolt, shining with a thousand eyes, the slayer of Vṛtra (वृत्रप्राणहरे चैन्द्रि) — Indra’s own deed ascribed to his Śakti.

8.22Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

ततः परिवृतस्ताभिरीशानो देवशक्तिभिः।
हन्यन्तामसुराः शीघ्रं मम प्रीत्याऽऽह चण्डिकाम् ॥ २२ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

ततः | परिवृतः | ताभिः | ईशानः | देवशक्तिभिःहन्यन्ताम् | असुराः | शीघ्रम् | मम | प्रीत्या | आह | चण्डिकाम्

3IAST

As recitedtataḥ parivṛtastābhirīśāno devaśaktibhiḥ |
hanyantāmasurāḥ śīghraṃ mama prītyā''ha caṇḍikām ||

Separatedtataḥ | parivṛtaḥ | tābhiḥ | īśānaḥ | devaśaktibhiḥ
hanyantām | asurāḥ | śīghram | mama | prītyā | āha | caṇḍikām

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
परिवृतःparivṛtaḥsurroundedpast passive participle (kta) of परि + √वृ (svādi 5), m. nom. sg.; qualifies ईशानः
ताभिःtābhiḥby thosepronoun तद्, f. instr. pl.; qualifies देवशक्तिभिः
ईशानःīśānaḥĪśāna (Śiva), the Lordm. nom. sg. of ईशान; subject of आह
देवशक्तिभिःdevaśaktibhiḥby the Śaktis of the godsf. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); agent with परिवृतः
हन्यन्ताम्hanyantāmlet (them) be slain√हन् (adādi 2P ‘to strike, kill’), karmaṇi prayoga, loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana
असुराःasurāḥthe asurasm. nom. pl. of असुर; subject of the passive हन्यन्ताम्
शीघ्रम्śīghramquicklyindeclinable (adverbial acc. of शीघ्र)
ममmamamyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; depends on प्रीत्या
प्रीत्याprītyāfor (my) pleasure, out of love (for me)f. instr. sg. of प्रीति; instrumental of cause
आहāhasaidthe defective verb आह ‘says, said’ (grammarians treat it as a substitute for √ब्रू in the present, with perfect endings), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; used of the past
चण्डिकाम्caṇḍikāmto Caṇḍikāf. acc. sg. of चण्डिका; the person addressed

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • परिवृतस्ताभिरीशानो parivṛtastābhirīśāno → परिवृतः + ताभिः + ईशानः
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66); हशि च (6.1.114)
    Visarga before t becomes s (परिवृतस्ताभिः); -iḥ before a vowel → -ir (ताभिरीशानः); -aḥ before the voiced d → -o (ईशानो देव-).
  • हन्यन्तामसुराः hanyantāmasurāḥ → हन्यन्ताम् + असुराः
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    Likewise शीघ्रं before m takes anusvāra (मोऽनुस्वारः).
  • प्रीत्याऽऽह prītyā''ha → प्रीत्या + आह
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + ā → ā. The double avagraha (ऽऽ) is an orthographic sign of the Gita Press: it marks that a long ā has been absorbed, so that the verb आह can be recognised.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • देवशक्तिभिः devaśaktibhiḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: देव + शक्ति · Vigraha: देवानां शक्तयः, ताभिः devānāṃ śaktayaḥ, tābhiḥ
    Literally “by the powers of the gods”; in the verse: the Mothers of 8.15–21

C. Morphology

  • हन्यन्ताम् hanyantām — Passive imperative: the passive stem हन्य- with the ātmanepada imperative ending -अन्ताम्. ‘Let the asuras be slain.’
  • आह āha — A defective verb found only in a few forms (आह, आहुः …), perfect in its endings, ‘said’. It introduces direct speech; here the speech comes first and आह follows it.

D. Anvaya — prose order

ततः ताभिः देवशक्तिभिः परिवृतः ईशानः चण्डिकाम् आह — “मम प्रीत्या असुराः शीघ्रं हन्यन्ताम्” (इति)।

tataḥ tābhiḥ devaśaktibhiḥ parivṛtaḥ īśānaḥ caṇḍikām āha — “mama prītyā asurāḥ śīghraṃ hanyantām” (iti) |

A main clause (ईशानः चण्डिकाम् आह) with the participle phrase परिवृतः … देवशक्तिभिः; the quoted request is a passive imperative.

6Translation

LiteralThen Īśāna, surrounded by those Śaktis of the gods, said to Caṇḍikā: “Let the asuras be slain quickly, for my pleasure.”

ReadableThen Śiva, surrounded by those Śaktis of the gods, said to Caṇḍikā, “Let the asuras be slain at once, for my sake.”

Īśāna is rendered as Śiva; मम प्रीत्या, ‘for my pleasure’, is rendered ‘for my sake’.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningŚiva (Īśāna) is on the field, among the Śaktis, and asks Caṇḍikā to destroy the asuras quickly. He does not fight; he asks.

Traditional interpretationOf the gods whose Śaktis came, only Śiva appears in person. His request opens the episode in which the Goddess sends him, of all the gods, on an errand (8.24–28).

1Original śloka

ततो देवीशरीरात्तु विनिष्क्रान्तातिभीषणा।

caṇḍikāśaktiratyugrā śivāśataninādinī ||

caṇḍikāśaktiḥ | atyugrā | śivāśataninādinī
