5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • ततो देवीशरीरात्तु tato devīśarīrāttu → ततः + देवीशरीरात् + तु
    visarga sandhi; consonant (no change) · हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87); खरि च (8.4.55)
    -aḥ before the voiced d becomes -o; the final t of शरीरात् stays t before t (तु).
  • विनिष्क्रान्तातिभीषणा viniṣkrāntātibhīṣaṇā → विनिष्क्रान्ता + अतिभीषणा
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + a → ā.
  • चण्डिकाशक्तिरत्युग्रा caṇḍikāśaktiratyugrā → चण्डिकाशक्तिः + अत्युग्रा
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    -iḥ before a vowel → -ir. Inside अत्युग्रा, अति + उग्रा → अत्युग्रा (इको यणचि, 6.1.77).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • देवीशरीरात् devīśarīrāt — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: देवी + शरीर · Vigraha: देव्याः शरीरम्, तस्मात् devyāḥ śarīram, tasmāt
    Literally “from the body of the Goddess”; in the verse: from Caṇḍikā herself
  • अतिभीषणा atibhīṣaṇā — prādi tatpuruṣa
    Members: अति + भीषण · Vigraha: अतिशयेन भीषणा atiśayena bhīṣaṇā
    Literally “exceedingly terrible”; in the verse: dreadful
  • चण्डिकाशक्तिः caṇḍikāśaktiḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: चण्डिका + शक्ति · Vigraha: चण्डिकायाः शक्तिः caṇḍikāyāḥ śaktiḥ
    Literally “the power of Caṇḍikā”; in the verse: the Goddess’s own Śakti, beside the gods’ Śaktis
  • अत्युग्रा atyugrā — prādi tatpuruṣa
    Members: अति + उग्र · Vigraha: अतिशयेन उग्रा atiśayena ugrā
    Literally “exceedingly fierce”; in the verse: most fierce
  • शिवाशतनिनादिनी śivāśataninādinī — upapada tatpuruṣa (the agent suffix -इन् with the standard of comparison as first member), containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: शिवाशत + निनादिन् · Vigraha: शिवाशतम् इव निनदति इति (शिवाशतम् = शिवानां शतम्) śivāśatam iva ninadati iti (śivāśatam = śivānāṃ śatam)
    Literally “she who howls like a hundred jackals”; in the verse: her cry is the howl of a pack of jackals
    Alternative: Or a bahuvrīhi in sense, ‘she whose cry is that of a hundred jackals’; the meaning is the same.

C. Morphology

  • विनिष्क्रान्ता viniṣkrāntā — Past participle of वि + निस् + √क्रम् used as a finite verb: ‘came forth’. Kālī ‘came forth’ (विनिष्क्रान्ता) from the Goddess’s brow with the same word at 7.6.
  • शिवाशतनिनादिनी śivāśataninādinī — शिवा (feminine) is the jackal, ‘the auspicious one’ by euphemism. The word prepares the name Śivadūtī (8.28) and the ‘jackals’ of 8.27.

D. Anvaya — prose order

ततः तु देवीशरीरात् अतिभीषणा अत्युग्रा शिवाशतनिनादिनी चण्डिकाशक्तिः विनिष्क्रान्ता।

tataḥ tu devīśarīrāt atibhīṣaṇā atyugrā śivāśataninādinī caṇḍikāśaktiḥ viniṣkrāntā |

Subject (चण्डिकाशक्तिः) with three qualifiers; source (देवीशरीरात्); verb (विनिष्क्रान्ता).

6Translation

LiteralThen from the Goddess’s body came forth the Śakti of Caṇḍikā, most terrible, exceedingly fierce, howling like a hundred jackals.

ReadableThen from the body of the Goddess there came forth Caṇḍikā’s Śakti — most terrible, exceedingly fierce, howling like a hundred jackals.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe gods have put forth their Śaktis; now the Goddess puts forth her own. She will be named Śivadūtī in 8.28.

Traditional interpretationJackals haunt the battlefield and the cremation ground, and their howls are ill-omened. The Śakti’s jackal-cry marks her as a power of the battlefield; her terrible laughter again frightens the asuras at 9.23.

8.24Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

सा चाह धूम्रजटिलमीशानमपराजिता।
दूत त्वं गच्छ भगवन् पार्श्वं शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः ॥ २४ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

सा | च | आह | धूम्रजटिलम् | ईशानम् | अपराजितादूत | त्वम् | गच्छ | भगवन् | पार्श्वम् | शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः

3IAST

As recitedsā cāha dhūmrajaṭilamīśānamaparājitā |
dūta tvaṃ gaccha bhagavan pārśvaṃ śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ ||

Separatedsā | ca | āha | dhūmrajaṭilam | īśānam | aparājitā
dūta | tvam | gaccha | bhagavan | pārśvam | śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of आह
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
आहāhasaiddefective verb आह, prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada (as in 8.22)
धूम्रजटिलम्dhūmrajaṭilamsmoke-grey and with matted locksm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies ईशानम्
ईशानम्īśānamĪśāna (Śiva)m. acc. sg. of ईशान; the person addressed
अपराजिताaparājitāthe Unconqueredf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies सा
दूतdūtaO messengerm. voc. sg. of दूत
त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of गच्छ
गच्छgacchago√गम् (bhvādi 1P, present stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
भगवन्bhagavanO Lordm. voc. sg. of भगवत्
पार्श्वम्pārśvamto the side (of), to the presence (of)n. acc. sg. of पार्श्व; accusative of goal
शुम्भनिशुम्भयोःśumbhaniśumbhayoḥof Śumbha and Niśumbham. gen. du.; compound (see Samāsa); depends on पार्श्वम्

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • सा चाह sā cāha → सा + च + आह
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā.
  • धूम्रजटिलमीशानमपराजिता dhūmrajaṭilamīśānamaparājitā → धूम्रजटिलम् + ईशानम् + अपराजिता
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    Both final m’s are kept and written together with the following vowels.
  • त्वं गच्छ tvaṃ gaccha → त्वम् + गच्छ
    anusvāra · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra; so too पार्श्वं शुम्भ-.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • धूम्रजटिलम् dhūmrajaṭilam — karmadhāraya (both members qualifiers)
    Members: धूम्र + जटिल · Vigraha: धूम्रः चासौ जटिलः च, तम् dhūmraḥ cāsau jaṭilaḥ ca, tam
    Literally “smoke-coloured and matted-haired”; in the verse: ash-grey Śiva with his matted locks
    Alternative: Or ‘with smoke-grey matted locks’, taking धूम्र with the locks implied in जटिल; the picture is the same.
  • अपराजिता aparājitā — nañ tatpuruṣa
    Members: अ + पराजित · Vigraha: न पराजिता na parājitā
    Literally “not conquered”; in the verse: the Invincible, an epithet of the Goddess’s Śakti
  • शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ — itaretara dvandva (dual)
    Members: शुम्भ + निशुम्भ · Vigraha: शुम्भः च निशुम्भः च, तयोः śumbhaḥ ca niśumbhaḥ ca, tayoḥ
    Literally “of Śumbha and Niśumbha”; in the verse: of the two asura brothers

C. Morphology

  • दूत dūta — Vocative, not a predicate: the nominative would be दूतः (दूतस्त्वम्). She addresses Śiva by his new office: ‘Messenger, go!’
  • भगवन् bhagavan — Vocative of भगवत्, ‘O Blessed One’, with the strong stem’s n.
  • गच्छ gaccha — Imperative second singular of √गम् with the present stem गच्छ- and no ending.

D. Anvaya — prose order

अपराजिता सा च धूम्रजटिलम् ईशानम् आह — “भगवन्, दूत, त्वं शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः पार्श्वं गच्छ।”

aparājitā sā ca dhūmrajaṭilam īśānam āha — “bhagavan, dūta, tvaṃ śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ pārśvaṃ gaccha | ”

The narrative frame (सा … ईशानम् आह) and the start of her speech: two vocatives and an imperative with its goal.

6Translation

LiteralAnd she, the Unconquered, said to Īśāna, smoke-grey and matted-haired: “Messenger, go, O Lord, to the side of Śumbha and Niśumbha.

ReadableAnd she, the Unconquered, said to the ash-grey, matted-haired Śiva: “Go, Lord, as my messenger to Śumbha and Niśumbha.

The vocative दूत is rendered ‘as my messenger’; ‘my’ is supplied.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe Goddess’s new Śakti makes Śiva himself her envoy to the asura kings. The speech runs to 8.27.

Traditional interpretationThe scene reverses chapter 5, where Śumbha sent the asura Sugrīva as messenger to the Goddess (5.102). Now the Goddess sends the highest of the gods to Śumbha — in the Śākta reading, a sign that Śiva acts at the bidding of Śakti.

8.25Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

ब्रूहि शुम्भं निशुम्भं च दानवावतिगर्वितौ।
ये चान्ये दानवास्तत्र युद्धाय समुपस्थिताः ॥ २५ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

ब्रूहि | शुम्भम् | निशुम्भम् | च | दानवौ | अतिगर्वितौये | च | अन्ये | दानवाः | तत्र | युद्धाय | समुपस्थिताः

3IAST

As recitedbrūhi śumbhaṃ niśumbhaṃ ca dānavāvatigarvitau |
ye cānye dānavāstatra yuddhāya samupasthitāḥ ||

Separatedbrūhi | śumbham | niśumbham | ca | dānavau | atigarvitau
ye | ca | anye | dānavāḥ | tatra | yuddhāya | samupasthitāḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
ब्रूहिbrūhisay (to), tell√ब्रू (adādi 2P ‘to speak’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
शुम्भम्śumbhamŚumbham. acc. sg.; the person addressed (ब्रू takes the person in the accusative)
निशुम्भम्niśumbhamNiśumbham. acc. sg.; the person addressed
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
दानवौdānavauthe two Dānavasm. acc. du. of दानव; in apposition to शुम्भम् and निशुम्भम्
अतिगर्वितौatigarvitauexceedingly proudm. acc. du.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies दानवौ
येyewhorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. pl.
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
अन्येanyeotherpronominal adjective अन्य, m. nom. pl.; qualifies दानवाः
दानवाःdānavāḥDānavasm. nom. pl. of दानव; subject of the relative clause
तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place)
युद्धायyuddhāyafor battlen. dat. sg. of युद्ध; dative of purpose
समुपस्थिताःsamupasthitāḥassembled, presentpast participle (kta) of सम् + उप + √स्था, m. nom. pl.; predicate of the relative clause

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • दानवावतिगर्वितौ dānavāvatigarvitau → दानवौ + अतिगर्वितौ
    vowel sandhi (ayādi) · एचोऽयवायावः (6.1.78)
    au before a vowel becomes āv: दानवौ + अ- → दानवाव-.
  • चान्ये cānye → च + अन्ये
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.
  • दानवास्तत्र dānavāstatra → दानवाः + तत्र
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)
    Visarga before t becomes s.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • अतिगर्वितौ atigarvitau — prādi tatpuruṣa
    Members: अति + गर्वित · Vigraha: अतिशयेन गर्वितौ atiśayena garvitau
    Literally “exceedingly proud”; in the verse: arrogant

C. Morphology

  • ब्रूहि brūhi — Imperative second singular of √ब्रू with the ending -हि; the root takes the person spoken to in the accusative (शुम्भं निशुम्भं च).
  • दानवौ dānavau — Dual: the brothers are treated as a pair. ‘Dānava’ (son of Danu) and ‘Daitya’ (son of Diti) are used for the same asuras throughout.

D. Anvaya — prose order

अतिगर्वितौ दानवौ शुम्भं निशुम्भं च ब्रूहि, ये च अन्ये दानवाः तत्र युद्धाय समुपस्थिताः (तान् च ब्रूहि) —

atigarvitau dānavau śumbhaṃ niśumbhaṃ ca brūhi, ye ca anye dānavāḥ tatra yuddhāya samupasthitāḥ (tān ca brūhi) —

The imperative ब्रूहि with two named addressees; a relative clause adds the rest of the host, whose correlative (तान्) is understood.

6Translation

LiteralSay to Śumbha and Niśumbha, the two exceedingly proud Dānavas, and (to) the other Dānavas who are assembled there for battle:

ReadableSay to Śumbha and Niśumbha, those two arrogant Dānavas, and to all the other Dānavas gathered there for battle:

The correlative ‘to those’ is supplied for the relative clause.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe message is addressed to the two kings and to the whole host. Its words follow in 8.26–27.

Traditional interpretationLike a royal embassy, the message is spoken to the rulers and their assembled vassals alike: it is an ultimatum, and all who hear it are bound by their answer.

8.26Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

त्रैलोक्यमिन्द्रो लभतां देवाः सन्तु हविर्भुजः।
यूयं प्रयात पातालं यदि जीवितुमिच्छथ ॥ २६ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

त्रैलोक्यम् | इन्द्रः | लभताम् | देवाः | सन्तु | हविर्भुजःयूयम् | प्रयात | पातालम् | यदि | जीवितुम् | इच्छथ

3IAST

As recitedtrailokyamindro labhatāṃ devāḥ santu havirbhujaḥ |
yūyaṃ prayāta pātālaṃ yadi jīvitumicchatha ||

Separatedtrailokyam | indraḥ | labhatām | devāḥ | santu | havirbhujaḥ
yūyam | prayāta | pātālam | yadi | jīvitum | icchatha

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
त्रैलोक्यम्trailokyamthe three worldsn. acc. sg. of त्रैलोक्य (taddhita from त्रिलोक); object of लभताम्
इन्द्रःindraḥIndram. nom. sg. of इन्द्र; subject of लभताम्
लभताम्labhatāmlet (him) obtain, let (him) have√लभ् (bhvādi 1Ā ‘to obtain’), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada
देवाःdevāḥthe godsm. nom. pl. of देव; subject of सन्तु
सन्तुsantulet (them) be√अस् (adādi 2P ‘to be’), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
हविर्भुजःhavirbhujaḥenjoyers of the oblationsm. nom. pl. of the root-noun compound हविर्भुज्; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of सन्तु
यूयम्yūyamyou (all)second-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. pl.; subject of प्रयात
प्रयातprayātago away, departप्र + √या (adādi 2P), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
पातालम्pātālamto Pātāla, the underworldn. acc. sg. of पाताल; accusative of goal
यदिyadiifindeclinable (conditional conjunction)
जीवितुम्jīvitumto liveinfinitive (tumun) of √जीव् (bhvādi 1P ‘to live’)
इच्छथicchathayou wish√इष् (tudādi 6P ‘to wish’, present stem इच्छ-), laṭ (present), madhyama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • त्रैलोक्यमिन्द्रो लभतां trailokyamindro labhatāṃ → त्रैलोक्यम् + इन्द्रः + लभताम्
    final m before a vowel; visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    The m stays before the vowel of इन्द्रः; -aḥ before the voiced l becomes -o.
  • देवाः सन्तु devāḥ santu → देवाः + सन्तु
    visarga sandhi (no change) · खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)
    Before a sibilant the visarga may stay (the alternative is assimilation, देवास्सन्तु).
  • हविर्भुजः havirbhujaḥ → हविस् + भुजः
    visarga sandhi inside a compound · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    The s of हविस् becomes r before the voiced bh.
  • जीवितुमिच्छथ jīvitumicchatha → जीवितुम् + इच्छथ
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    Elsewhere in the verse final m becomes anusvāra before consonants (यूयं प्रयात, पातालं यदि).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • हविर्भुजः havirbhujaḥ — upapada tatpuruṣa
    Members: हविस् + भुज् · Vigraha: हवींषि भुञ्जते इति हविर्भुजः havīṃṣi bhuñjate iti havirbhujaḥ
    Literally “eaters of oblations”; in the verse: the gods restored to their share in the sacrifice

C. Morphology

  • त्रैलोक्यम् trailokyam — A taddhita in -य with vṛddhi (त्रि → त्रै) on त्रिलोक: ‘the triad of worlds’, a collective neuter singular.
  • लभताम् labhatām — Third-person imperative, ātmanepada (√लभ् is ātmanepada): ‘let him obtain’.
  • इच्छथ icchatha — √इष् forms its present stem इच्छ- (the root’s ṣ becomes cch): इच्छथ ‘you wish’.
  • हविर्भुजः havirbhujaḥ — भुज् is a root used as a noun at the end of a compound; consonant stem, nominative plural -अः.

D. Anvaya — prose order

इन्द्रः त्रैलोक्यं लभताम्; देवाः हविर्भुजः सन्तु; यूयं यदि जीवितुम् इच्छथ, (तर्हि) पातालं प्रयात।

indraḥ trailokyaṃ labhatām; devāḥ havirbhujaḥ santu; yūyaṃ yadi jīvitum icchatha, (tarhi) pātālaṃ prayāta |

Three imperatives: two third-person (for Indra and the gods), one second-person (for the asuras) with a conditional clause.

6Translation

LiteralLet Indra obtain the three worlds; let the gods be enjoyers of the oblations. You — go to Pātāla, if you wish to live.

Readable‘Let Indra have the three worlds, and let the gods receive the sacrificial offerings. As for you, go down to Pātāla, if you wish to live.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe terms of peace: Indra’s sovereignty and the gods’ share of the sacrifice are to be restored, and the asuras are to withdraw to the underworld.

Traditional interpretationThe terms answer the wrong of 5.2, where Śumbha and Niśumbha took from Indra the three worlds and the shares of the sacrifice (त्रैलोक्यं यज्ञभागाश्च हृताः), and Śumbha’s boast at 5.108. They are fulfilled at the end of the war: the gods again enjoy their shares (12.34) and the surviving asuras go to Pātāla (12.35).

8.27Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

बलावलेपादथ चेद्भवन्तो युद्धकाङ्क्षिणः।
तदागच्छत तृप्यन्तु मच्छिवाः पिशितेन वः ॥ २७ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

बलावलेपात् | अथ | चेत् | भवन्तः | युद्धकाङ्क्षिणःतत् | आगच्छत | तृप्यन्तु | मच्छिवाः | पिशितेन | वः

3IAST

As recitedbalāvalepādatha cedbhavanto yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ |
tadāgacchata tṛpyantu macchivāḥ piśitena vaḥ ||

Separatedbalāvalepāt | atha | cet | bhavantaḥ | yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ
tat | āgacchata | tṛpyantu | macchivāḥ | piśitena | vaḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
बलावलेपात्balāvalepātout of pride in (your) strengthm. abl. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); ablative of cause
अथathabut, nowindeclinable (particle introducing the alternative)
चेत्cetifindeclinable (conditional particle)
भवन्तःbhavantaḥyou (sirs)honorific pronoun भवत्, m. nom. pl.; subject (supply स्थ or सन्ति)
युद्धकाङ्क्षिणःyuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥdesirous of battlem. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of भवन्तः
तत्tatthen, in that caseindeclinable (correlative of चेत्; अव्यय use of तद्)
आगच्छतāgacchatacomeआ + √गम् (bhvādi 1P, stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
तृप्यन्तुtṛpyantulet (them) be sated√तृप् (divādi 4P ‘to be satisfied’), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
मच्छिवाःmacchivāḥmy jackalsf. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of तृप्यन्तु
पिशितेनpiśitenawith the fleshn. instr. sg. of पिशित; instrument of तृप्यन्तु
वःvaḥyoursecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, enclitic gen. pl.; depends on पिशितेन

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • बलावलेपादथ balāvalepādatha → बल + अवलेपात् + अथ
    vowel sandhi inside a compound; consonant sandhi (voicing) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)
    a + a → ā inside the compound; the final t of -लेपात् becomes d before the vowel of अथ.
  • चेद्भवन्तो cedbhavanto → चेत् + भवन्तः
    consonant sandhi (voicing); visarga sandhi · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39); हशि च (6.1.114)
    t becomes d before the voiced bh; -aḥ before the voiced y of युद्ध- becomes -o.
  • तदागच्छत tadāgacchata → तत् + आगच्छत
    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)
    Final t becomes d before a vowel. (The same letters could also be तदा + आगच्छत; see Morphology.)
  • मच्छिवाः macchivāḥ → मद् + शिवाः
    consonant sandhi inside a compound · स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40), शश्छोऽटि (8.4.63)
    t becomes c next to ś, and ś after the stop becomes ch: मत् + शिवाः → मच्छिवाः.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • बलावलेपात् balāvalepāt — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: बल + अवलेप · Vigraha: बलस्य अवलेपः, तस्मात् balasya avalepaḥ, tasmāt
    Literally “from the haughtiness of strength”; in the verse: out of arrogance in your might
  • युद्धकाङ्क्षिणः yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ — upapada tatpuruṣa
    Members: युद्ध + काङ्क्षिन् · Vigraha: युद्धं काङ्क्षन्ति इति yuddhaṃ kāṅkṣanti iti
    Literally “those who long for battle”; in the verse: spoiling for a fight
  • मच्छिवाः macchivāḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: मद् + शिवा · Vigraha: मम शिवाः mama śivāḥ
    Literally “my jackals”; in the verse: the jackals that attend the Goddess’s Śakti

C. Morphology

  • तत् tat — Taken as the correlative of चेत् (‘if …, then …’). The joined text तदागच्छत can equally be read तदा आगच्छत, ‘then come’; the meaning is the same.
  • भवन्तः bhavantaḥ — The honorific भवत् is grammatically third person, but the imperative that follows is second person (आगच्छत): the politeness of the address gives way to the challenge.
  • वः vaḥ — Enclitic form of the genitive plural of युष्मद् (= युष्माकम्); it never begins a sentence or pāda.

D. Anvaya — prose order

अथ चेत् भवन्तः बलावलेपात् युद्धकाङ्क्षिणः, तत् आगच्छत; मच्छिवाः वः पिशितेन तृप्यन्तु।

atha cet bhavantaḥ balāvalepāt yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ, tat āgacchata; macchivāḥ vaḥ piśitena tṛpyantu |

A conditional (चेत् …) with its main clause (तत् आगच्छत), and a closing imperative of the third person.

6Translation

LiteralBut if, out of the haughtiness of strength, you are desirous of battle, then come; let my jackals be sated with your flesh.”

ReadableBut if, proud of your strength, you long for battle, then come — and let my jackals feast on your flesh.’

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe alternative to withdrawal: battle, and the asuras’ flesh for the jackals. The message closes with the jackals that marked this Śakti’s cry (8.23).

Traditional interpretationThe charge of बलावलेप, arrogance in strength, is turned back on the Goddess by Śumbha at 10.3 (बलावलेपाद् दुष्टे त्वं मा दुर्गे गर्वमावह), where he accuses her of relying on others’ strength. Her answer there, that all these powers are her own (10.5), resolves both.

8.28Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

यतो नियुक्तो दौत्येन तया देव्या शिवः स्वयम्।
शिवदूतीति लोकेस्मिंस्ततः सा ख्यातिमागता ॥ २८ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

यतः | नियुक्तः | दौत्येन | तया | देव्या | शिवः | स्वयम्शिवदूती | इति | लोके | अस्मिन् | ततः | सा | ख्यातिम् | आगता

3IAST

As recitedyato niyukto dautyena tayā devyā śivaḥ svayam |
śivadūtīti lokesmiṃstataḥ sā khyātimāgatā ||

Separatedyataḥ | niyuktaḥ | dautyena | tayā | devyā | śivaḥ | svayam
śivadūtī | iti | loke | asmin | tataḥ | sā | khyātim | āgatā

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
यतःyataḥbecause, sinceindeclinable (relative adverb, यद् + tas); answered by ततः
नियुक्तःniyuktaḥwas appointedpast passive participle (kta) of नि + √युज् (rudhādi 7 ‘to join’; with नि ‘to appoint’), m. nom. sg.; predicate
दौत्येनdautyenato the office of messengern. instr. sg. of दौत्य (taddhita from दूत, ‘messengership’); instrument of the office
तयाtayāby thatpronoun तद्, f. instr. sg.; qualifies देव्या
देव्याdevyāby the Goddessf. instr. sg. of देवी; agent of the passive नियुक्तः
शिवःśivaḥŚivam. nom. sg. of शिव; subject of नियुक्तः
स्वयम्svayamhimselfindeclinable (reflexive adverb)
शिवदूतीśivadūtīŚivadūtī, ‘she whose messenger is Śiva’f. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); the name quoted by इति
इतिitias (the name)indeclinable (particle marking a quoted name)
लोकेlokein the worldm. loc. sg. of लोक
अस्मिन्asminthispronoun इदम्, m. loc. sg.; qualifies लोके
ततःtataḥthereforeindeclinable (correlative of यतः)
साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of आगता
ख्यातिम्khyātimrenown, famef. acc. sg. of ख्याति; object of आगता
आगताāgatāattained, came topast participle (kta) of आ + √गम्, f. nom. sg.; used as the finite verb

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • यतो नियुक्तो दौत्येन yato niyukto dautyena → यतः + नियुक्तः + दौत्येन
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    -aḥ before a voiced consonant becomes -o, twice.
  • शिवदूतीति śivadūtīti → शिवदूती + इति
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ī + i → ī.
  • लोकेस्मिंस्ततः lokesmiṃstataḥ → लोके + अस्मिन् + ततः
    vowel sandhi (pūrvarūpa); consonant sandhi (n before t) · एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109); नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7)
    -e + a- → -e: the a of अस्मिन् is elided (the Gita Press prints no avagraha here: लोकेस्मिन् for लोकेऽस्मिन्). Final n before t becomes ṃs: अस्मिंस्ततः.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • शिवदूती śivadūtī — bahuvrīhi
    Members: शिव + दूत · Vigraha: शिवः दूतः यस्याः सा śivaḥ dūtaḥ yasyāḥ sā
    Literally “she whose messenger is Śiva”; in the verse: the name given to Caṇḍikā’s Śakti for this deed
    Alternative: Formally it could be a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, शिवस्य दूती ‘Śiva’s (female) messenger’; the verse’s own explanation (Śiva was made messenger by her) requires the bahuvrīhi.

C. Morphology

  • दौत्येन dautyena — दौत्य, ‘the office of a दूत’, is a vṛddhi taddhita (दू → दौ). With नियुक्तः the office is put in the instrumental: ‘appointed with (= to) messengership’.
  • शिवदूती śivadūtī — A bahuvrīhi in दूत takes the feminine ई when it names a woman: शिवदूती.
  • आगता āgatā — Past participle used as the finite verb, with ख्यातिम् as object: ‘she came to fame’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

यतः तया देव्या शिवः स्वयं दौत्येन नियुक्तः, ततः सा अस्मिन् लोके “शिवदूती” इति ख्यातिम् आगता।

yataḥ tayā devyā śivaḥ svayaṃ dautyena niyuktaḥ, tataḥ sā asmin loke “śivadūtī” iti khyātim āgatā |

A causal correlative: यतः (passive clause: शिवः … देव्या नियुक्तः) … ततः (सा … ख्यातिम् आगता).

6Translation

LiteralBecause Śiva himself was appointed to the office of messenger by that Goddess, therefore she came to renown in this world as ‘Śivadūtī’.

ReadableBecause she made Śiva himself her messenger, the Goddess became known in this world as Śivadūtī — ‘she whose messenger is Śiva’.

The passive is turned active, and the meaning of the name is added in apposition.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe narrator explains the name: she is Śivadūtī because Śiva was her envoy. The formula follows the naming of Cāmuṇḍā (7.27: चामुण्डेति ततो लोके ख्याता).

Traditional interpretationŚivadūtī fights again in the next chapters (9.23, 9.37) and is praised in the hymn of chapter 11 (11.20: शिवदूतीस्वरूपेण हतदैत्यमहाबले).

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāThe Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) also has the name शिवदूती (name 405). The shared name is a real point of contact between the two Śākta works.

8.29Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

तेऽपि श्रुत्वा वचो देव्याः शर्वाख्यातं महासुराः।
अमर्षापूरिता जग्मुर्यत्र कात्यायनी स्थिता ॥ २९ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

ते | अपि | श्रुत्वा | वचः | देव्याः | शर्वाख्यातम् | महासुराःअमर्षापूरिताः | जग्मुः | यत्र | कात्यायनी | स्थिता

3IAST

As recitedte'pi śrutvā vaco devyāḥ śarvākhyātaṃ mahāsurāḥ |
amarṣāpūritā jagmuryatra kātyāyanī sthitā ||

Separatedte | api | śrutvā | vacaḥ | devyāḥ | śarvākhyātam | mahāsurāḥ
amarṣāpūritāḥ | jagmuḥ | yatra | kātyāyanī | sthitā

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
तेtetheypronoun तद्, m. nom. pl.; qualifies महासुराः
अपिapifor their part, tooindeclinable (particle)
श्रुत्वाśrutvāhaving heardabsolutive (ktvā) of √श्रु (svādi 5P ‘to hear’)
वचःvacaḥthe words, the messagen. acc. sg. of वचस् (s-stem); object of श्रुत्वा
देव्याःdevyāḥof the Goddessf. gen. sg. of देवी; depends on वचः
शर्वाख्यातम्śarvākhyātamannounced by Śarva (Śiva)n. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies वचः
महासुराःmahāsurāḥthe great asurasm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of जग्मुः
अमर्षापूरिताःamarṣāpūritāḥfilled with indignationm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies महासुराः
जग्मुःjagmuḥwent√गम् (bhvādi 1P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
यत्रyatrawhereindeclinable (relative adverb of place)
कात्यायनीkātyāyanīKātyāyanī (the Goddess)f. nom. sg. of कात्यायनी; subject of स्थिता
स्थिताsthitāstoodpast participle (kta) of √स्था, f. nom. sg.; predicate of the relative clause

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • तेऽपि te'pi → ते + अपि
    vowel sandhi (pūrvarūpa) · एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109)
    -e + a- → -e’-: the a is elided and marked by the avagraha.
  • वचो देव्याः vaco devyāḥ → वचः + देव्याः
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    -aḥ before the voiced d becomes -o.
  • अमर्षापूरिता जग्मुर्यत्र amarṣāpūritā jagmuryatra → अमर्षापूरिताः + जग्मुः + यत्र
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17)
    -āḥ before a voiced consonant loses its visarga (-ā); -uḥ before the voiced y surfaces as -ur.
  • शर्वाख्यातं śarvākhyātaṃ → शर्व + आख्यातम्
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā; so too अमर्ष + आपूरिताः.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • शर्वाख्यातम् śarvākhyātam — tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa
    Members: शर्व + आख्यात · Vigraha: शर्वेण आख्यातम् śarveṇa ākhyātam
    Literally “told by Śarva”; in the verse: the message as Śiva delivered it
  • महासुराः mahāsurāḥ — karmadhāraya
    Members: महत् + असुर · Vigraha: महान्तः च ते असुराः च mahāntaḥ ca te asurāḥ ca
    Literally “great asuras”; in the verse: the asura chiefs
  • अमर्षापूरिताः amarṣāpūritāḥ — tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa
    Members: अमर्ष + आपूरित · Vigraha: अमर्षेण आपूरिताः amarṣeṇa āpūritāḥ
    Literally “filled with intolerance”; in the verse: seething with indignation

C. Morphology

  • जग्मुः jagmuḥ — Perfect third plural of √गम्: reduplication ज- and the weak root ग्म्- (the vowel drops before -उः).
  • कात्यायनी kātyāyanī — A patronymic name of the Goddess, ‘she of the Kātyāyana line’; its sense in this text is simply a name of the Goddess, used again in 11.2 and 11.25.

D. Anvaya — prose order

ते महासुराः अपि शर्वाख्यातं देव्याः वचः श्रुत्वा अमर्षापूरिताः (सन्तः), यत्र कात्यायनी स्थिता, (तत्र) जग्मुः।

te mahāsurāḥ api śarvākhyātaṃ devyāḥ vacaḥ śrutvā amarṣāpūritāḥ (santaḥ), yatra kātyāyanī sthitā, (tatra) jagmuḥ |

Subject (ते महासुराः) with an absolutive clause and a qualifier; main verb जग्मुः with a relative clause of place (यत्र … स्थिता).

6Translation

LiteralThey, the great asuras, for their part, having heard the Goddess’s words announced by Śarva, filled with indignation, went where Kātyāyanī stood.

ReadableAnd the great asuras, hearing the Goddess’s message as Śiva delivered it, went, seething with indignation, to where Kātyāyanī stood.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe asuras reject the terms and march on the Goddess. Śiva is here called Śarva; the Goddess, Kātyāyanī.

Traditional interpretationAs with the earlier messages (5.122 onward, 6.2), the answer to the Goddess’s word is indignation (अमर्ष) and attack. The tradition reads the pattern as the ego refusing the one offer that would save it.

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāThe Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has the name कात्यायनी (name 556). The two works share the name; their stories are separate.

8.30Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

ततः प्रथममेवाग्रे शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिः।
ववर्षुरुद्धतामर्षास्तां देवीममरारयः ॥ ३० ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

ततः | प्रथमम् | एव | अग्रे | शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिःववर्षुः | उद्धतामर्षाः | ताम् | देवीम् | अमरारयः

3IAST

As recitedtataḥ prathamamevāgre śaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥ |
vavarṣuruddhatāmarṣāstāṃ devīmamarārayaḥ ||

Separatedtataḥ | prathamam | eva | agre | śaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥ
vavarṣuḥ | uddhatāmarṣāḥ | tām | devīm | amarārayaḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
प्रथमम्prathamamfirst, at the outsetindeclinable (adverbial acc. of प्रथम)
एवevaat once, the very (first)indeclinable (emphatic particle)
अग्रेagrein front, in the vann. loc. sg. of अग्र, used adverbially
शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिःśaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥwith showers of arrows, spears and swordsf. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument of ववर्षुः
ववर्षुःvavarṣuḥrained (upon)√वृष् (bhvādi 1P ‘to rain’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
उद्धतामर्षाःuddhatāmarṣāḥtheir fury rousedm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अमरारयः
ताम्tāmthatpronoun तद्, f. acc. sg.; qualifies देवीम्
देवीम्devīmthe Goddessf. acc. sg. of देवी; object of ववर्षुः
अमरारयःamarārayaḥthe enemies of the immortalsm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of ववर्षुः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • प्रथममेवाग्रे prathamamevāgre → प्रथमम् + एव + अग्रे
    final m before a vowel; vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    The m is kept before the vowel of एव; a + a → ā (एवाग्रे).
  • शक्त्यृष्टि śaktyṛṣṭi → शक्ति + ऋष्टि
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    i before the vowel ṛ becomes y.
  • ववर्षुरुद्धतामर्षास्तां vavarṣuruddhatāmarṣāstāṃ → ववर्षुः + उद्धतामर्षाः + ताम्
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66); विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)
    -uḥ before a vowel → -ur; -āḥ before t → -ās.
  • देवीममरारयः devīmamarārayaḥ → देवीम् + अमरारयः
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    Inside the compound अमर + अरयः → अमरारयः (a + a → ā, 6.1.101).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिः śaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa whose first member is an itaretara dvandva
    Members: शर + शक्ति + ऋष्टि + वृष्टि · Vigraha: शराः च शक्तयः च ऋष्टयः च शरशक्त्यृष्टयः; तेषां वृष्टयः, ताभिः śarāḥ ca śaktayaḥ ca ṛṣṭayaḥ ca śaraśaktyṛṣṭayaḥ; teṣāṃ vṛṣṭayaḥ, tābhiḥ
    Literally “with rains of arrows, spears and swords”; in the verse: with volleys of every missile
  • उद्धतामर्षाः uddhatāmarṣāḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: उद्धत + अमर्ष · Vigraha: उद्धतः अमर्षः येषां ते uddhataḥ amarṣaḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally “those whose indignation is roused”; in the verse: inflamed with rage
  • अमरारयः amarārayaḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: अमर + अरि · Vigraha: अमराणाम् अरयः amarāṇām arayaḥ
    Literally “enemies of the immortals”; in the verse: the asuras

C. Morphology

  • ववर्षुः vavarṣuḥ — Perfect third plural of √वृष् (reduplication व-वर्ष्-). ‘To rain’ takes the target in the accusative: तां देवीं ववर्षुः ‘they rained upon the Goddess’.
  • अमरारयः amarārayaḥ — Nominative plural of the i-stem अरि with guṇa: -अयः.

D. Anvaya — prose order

ततः उद्धतामर्षाः अमरारयः प्रथमम् एव अग्रे तां देवीं शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिः ववर्षुः।

tataḥ uddhatāmarṣāḥ amarārayaḥ prathamam eva agre tāṃ devīṃ śaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥ vavarṣuḥ |

Subject (अमरारयः) with its qualifier, adverbs of time and place, object, instrument, verb.

6Translation

LiteralThen at the very first, in front, the enemies of the immortals, their indignation roused, rained upon that Goddess with showers of arrows, spears and swords.

ReadableThen the enemies of the gods, their fury roused, at once rained down on the Goddess from the front showers of arrows, spears and swords.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe asuras strike first, answering the message with a volley of missiles. ऋष्टि is a sword or two-edged blade; शक्ति here is the hurled spear.

Traditional interpretationThe image of weapons as rain recurs in the next chapter, where Śumbha and Niśumbha pour arrows on the Goddess ‘like two clouds raining’ (9.9: मेघयोरिव वर्षतोः).

8.31Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

सा च तान् प्रहितान् बाणाञ्छूलशक्तिपरश्वधान्।
चिच्छेद लीलयाऽऽध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैर्महेषुभिः ॥ ३१ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

सा | च | तान् | प्रहितान् | बाणान् | शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान्चिच्छेद | लीलया | आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैः | महेषुभिः

3IAST

As recitedsā ca tān prahitān bāṇāñchūlaśaktiparaśvadhān |
ciccheda līlayā''dhmātadhanurmuktairmaheṣubhiḥ ||

Separatedsā | ca | tān | prahitān | bāṇān | śūlaśaktiparaśvadhān
ciccheda | līlayā | ādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥ | maheṣubhiḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of चिच्छेद
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
तान्tānthosepronoun तद्, m. acc. pl.; qualifies बाणान् and शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान्
प्रहितान्prahitānhurled, launchedpast passive participle (kta) of प्र + √हि (svādi 5P ‘to impel’), m. acc. pl.
बाणान्bāṇānarrowsm. acc. pl. of बाण; object of चिच्छेद
शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान्śūlaśaktiparaśvadhānpikes, spears and axesm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); object of चिच्छेद
चिच्छेदcicchedacut (to pieces)√छिद् (rudhādi 7P ‘to cut’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
लीलयाlīlayāwith ease, as if in playf. instr. sg. of लीला; instrumental of manner
आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैःādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥloosed from her resounding bowm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies महेषुभिः
महेषुभिःmaheṣubhiḥwith great arrowsm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument of चिच्छेद

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • बाणाञ्छूल bāṇāñchūla → बाणान् + शूल-
    consonant sandhi (n before ś) · स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40), शश्छोऽटि (8.4.63)
    n becomes the palatal ñ next to ś, and the ś may then become ch (the change of 8.4.63, which a vārttika extends to follow a nasal): -ān + śū- → -āñchū-.
  • लीलयाऽऽध्मात līlayā''dhmāta → लीलया + आध्मात-
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + ā → ā; the double avagraha is the Gita Press sign that a long ā was absorbed, as in प्रीत्याऽऽह (8.22).
  • धनुर्मुक्तैर्महेषुभिः dhanurmuktairmaheṣubhiḥ → धनुस् + मुक्तैः + महेषुभिः
    visarga sandhi (inside the compound and between words) · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    धनुस् before the voiced m becomes धनुर्; -aiḥ before m becomes -air.
  • महेषुभिः maheṣubhiḥ → महा + इषुभिः
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    ā + i → e.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान् śūlaśaktiparaśvadhān — itaretara dvandva
    Members: शूल + शक्ति + परश्वध · Vigraha: शूलाः च शक्तयः च परश्वधाः च, तान् śūlāḥ ca śaktayaḥ ca paraśvadhāḥ ca, tān
    Literally “pikes and spears and axes”; in the verse: every kind of hurled weapon
  • आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैः ādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥ — pañcamī tatpuruṣa containing a karmadhāraya
    Members: आध्मात + धनुस् + मुक्त · Vigraha: आध्मातं धनुः आध्मातधनुः (karmadhāraya); आध्मातधनुषः मुक्ताः, तैः ādhmātaṃ dhanuḥ ādhmātadhanuḥ (karmadhāraya); ādhmātadhanuṣaḥ muktāḥ, taiḥ
    Literally “released from the blown (resounding) bow”; in the verse: shot from her thrumming bow
  • महेषुभिः maheṣubhiḥ — karmadhāraya
    Members: महत् + इषु · Vigraha: महान्तः च ते इषवः च, तैः mahāntaḥ ca te iṣavaḥ ca, taiḥ
    Literally “with great arrows”; in the verse: with mighty shafts

C. Morphology

  • चिच्छेद ciccheda — Perfect of √छिद्: the reduplicated syllable has c for ch (चि-), and ch after a short vowel is doubled to cch (चिच्छेद).
  • आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैः ādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥ — आध्मात is the past participle of आ + √ध्मा ‘to blow’: the bow is ‘blown’, i.e. made to resound as it is drawn and loosed.
  • प्रहितान् prahitān — Past participle of प्र + √हि ‘to send forth’: the weapons the asuras had hurled (8.30).
  • शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान् śūlaśaktiparaśvadhān — An itaretara dvandva takes the gender of its last member: परश्वध is masculine, so the accusative plural is -आन्, agreeing with तान् प्रहितान्.

D. Anvaya — prose order

सा च आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैः महेषुभिः तान् प्रहितान् बाणान् शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान् (च) लीलया चिच्छेद।

sā ca ādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥ maheṣubhiḥ tān prahitān bāṇān śūlaśaktiparaśvadhān (ca) līlayā ciccheda |

Subject, instrument (महेषुभिः with its qualifier), two objects qualified by तान् प्रहितान्, adverb of manner, verb.

6Translation

LiteralAnd she, with great arrows loosed from her resounding bow, cut with ease those launched arrows, pikes, spears and axes.

ReadableAnd with great arrows loosed from her thrumming bow she cut down, as if in play, the arrows, pikes, spears and axes they had hurled.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe Goddess cuts every missile in the air. The word लीलया, ‘in play’, is the narrator’s mark of her ease.

Traditional interpretationThe same word marks her battles in every carita: she cuts the weapons ‘as if in play’ at 2.50 and 3.4, and breaks Śumbha’s weapons so at 10.13 (लीलयैव). The tradition reads the divine acts of creation and destruction as her play (līlā).

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāThe Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) gives the idea a cosmic scale: name 648, लीलाकॢप्तब्रह्माण्डमण्डला, ‘who fashioned the sphere of the universe in play’.

8.32Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

तस्याग्रतस्तथा काली शूलपातविदारितान्।
खट्वाङ्गपोथितांश्चारीन् कुर्वती व्यचरत्तदा ॥ ३२ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

तस्य | अग्रतः | तथा | काली | शूलपातविदारितान्खट्वाङ्गपोथितान् | च | अरीन् | कुर्वती | व्यचरत् | तदा

3IAST

As recitedtasyāgratastathā kālī śūlapātavidāritān |
khaṭvāṅgapothitāṃścārīn kurvatī vyacarattadā ||

Separatedtasya | agrataḥ | tathā | kālī | śūlapātavidāritān
khaṭvāṅgapothitān | ca | arīn | kurvatī | vyacarat | tadā

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
तस्यtasyaof it, of himpronoun तद्, m./n. gen. sg.; depends on अग्रतः
अग्रतःagrataḥin front (of)indeclinable (अग्र + tas, ‘at the front’), with the genitive
तथाtathālikewise, andindeclinable (adverb of manner)
कालीkālīKālīf. nom. sg. of काली; subject of व्यचरत्
शूलपातविदारितान्śūlapātavidāritāntorn by the blows of her spearm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate accusative with कुर्वती
खट्वाङ्गपोथितान्khaṭvāṅgapothitāncrushed by her skull-topped staffm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate accusative with कुर्वती
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
अरीन्arīnthe enemiesm. acc. pl. of अरि (i-stem); object of कुर्वती
कुर्वतीkurvatīmaking, renderingpresent participle (śatṛ) of √कृ (tanādi 8), f. nom. sg.; qualifies काली
व्यचरत्vyacaratranged about, moved through (the field)वि + √चर् (bhvādi 1P ‘to move’), laṅ (imperfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
तदाtadāthenindeclinable (adverb of time)

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • तस्याग्रतस्तथा tasyāgratastathā → तस्य + अग्रतः + तथा
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha); visarga sandhi · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)
    a + a → ā; visarga before t becomes s.
  • पोथितांश्चारीन् pothitāṃścārīn → पोथितान् + च + अरीन्
    consonant sandhi (n before c); vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    Final n before c becomes ṃś (पोथितांश्च); a + a → ā (चारीन्).
  • व्यचरत्तदा vyacarattadā → व्यचरत् + तदा
    consonant sandhi (no change) · खरि च (8.4.55)
    t stays t before t. Inside the verb, वि + अचरत् → व्यचरत् (इको यणचि, 6.1.77).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • शूलपातविदारितान् śūlapātavidāritān — tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (शूलपात)
    Members: शूलपात + विदारित · Vigraha: शूलस्य पाताः शूलपाताः; तैः विदारिताः, तान् śūlasya pātāḥ śūlapātāḥ; taiḥ vidāritāḥ, tān
    Literally “torn apart by the falls of the spear”; in the verse: ripped open by her spear-strokes
  • खट्वाङ्गपोथितान् khaṭvāṅgapothitān — tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa
    Members: खट्वाङ्ग + पोथित · Vigraha: खट्वाङ्गेन पोथिताः, तान् khaṭvāṅgena pothitāḥ, tān
    Literally “crushed by the khaṭvāṅga”; in the verse: battered with her skull-staff

C. Morphology

  • कुर्वती kurvatī — Feminine present participle of √कृ (stem कुर्वत्), with a double accusative: अरीन् (object) and the two participles (what she made them) — ‘making the enemies torn and crushed’.
  • व्यचरत् vyacarat — Imperfect with the augment between prefix and root: वि + अ + चरत्. Contrast the augmentless उपबृंहयत् of 8.9.
  • खट्वाङ्गपोथितान् khaṭvāṅgapothitān — पोथित is the past participle of the causative of √पुथ् ‘to crush’ (पोथयति). The khaṭvāṅga, a staff topped with a skull, is Kālī’s weapon from her first appearance (7.7, 7.15).

D. Anvaya — prose order

तदा काली तथा तस्य अग्रतः अरीन् शूलपातविदारितान् खट्वाङ्गपोथितान् च कुर्वती व्यचरत्।

tadā kālī tathā tasya agrataḥ arīn śūlapātavidāritān khaṭvāṅgapothitān ca kurvatī vyacarat |

Subject (काली) with the participle phrase कुर्वती (object अरीन्, two predicate accusatives); place (तस्य अग्रतः); verb व्यचरत्.

6Translation

LiteralLikewise Kālī then ranged in front of it, making the enemies torn by the blows of her spear and crushed by her khaṭvāṅga.

ReadableSo too Kālī ranged at the front of the fight, tearing the foe with blows of her spear and crushing them with her skull-staff.

तस्य अग्रतः, ‘in front of it/him’, is rendered ‘at the front of the fight’; see the flag.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningKālī fights in the van with spear and khaṭvāṅga. The Mothers’ deeds follow one by one (8.33–38).

Traditional interpretationThe khaṭvāṅga was Kālī’s weapon from her first appearance (7.7: विचित्रखट्वाङ्गधरा), and the asuras fell to it then as now (7.15: खट्वाङ्गताडिताः).

Textual Verification Required / uncertain: तस्य is masculine or neuter: most naturally the enemy host (सैन्यम्), at whose front Kālī ranges, or Śiva, last named in 8.29. Many renderings take it as ‘before her (Caṇḍikā)’, which would need तस्याः; that reading treats the masculine as a licence.

8.33Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपहतवीर्यान् हतौजसः।
ब्रह्माणी चाकरोच्छत्रून् येन येन स्म धावति ॥ ३३ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपहतवीर्यान् | हतौजसःब्रह्माणी | च | अकरोत् | शत्रून् | येन | येन | स्म | धावति
