येनyena(by whichever) wayrelative pronoun यद्, n. instr. sg.; the repetition (vīpsā) makes it distributive: ‘wherever’
स्मsma(past sense)indeclinable particle; with the present धावति it gives a past meaning
धावतिdhāvatishe ran, she moved√धाव् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to run’), laṭ (present) with स्म = past, prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; subject ब्रह्माणी

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • कमण्डलुजलाक्षेप kamaṇḍalujalākṣepa → कमण्डलुजल + आक्षेप
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā.
  • हतौजसः hataujasaḥ → हत + ओजसः
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    a + o → au: हत + ओजस् → हतौजस्.
  • चाकरोच्छत्रून् cākarocchatrūn → च + अकरोत् + शत्रून्
    vowel sandhi; consonant sandhi (t + ś) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40), शश्छोऽटि (8.4.63)
    च + अकरोत् → चाकरोत्. Then the final t, meeting ś, becomes the palatal c, and the ś after it becomes ch: अकरोत् शत्रून् → अकरोच्छत्रून्.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपहतवीर्यान् kamaṇḍalujalākṣepahatavīryān — bahuvrīhi containing ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣas (कमण्डलुजल, कमण्डलुजलाक्षेप) and a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (…आक्षेपहत)
    Members: कमण्डलु + जल + आक्षेप + हत + वीर्य · Vigraha: कमण्डलोः जलं कमण्डलुजलम्; तस्य आक्षेपः कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपः; तेन हतं वीर्यं येषां ते, तान् kamaṇḍaloḥ jalaṃ kamaṇḍalujalam; tasya ākṣepaḥ kamaṇḍalujalākṣepaḥ; tena hataṃ vīryaṃ yeṣāṃ te, tān
    Literally “those whose valour was struck down by the throwing of water-pot water”; in the verse: enemies robbed of their strength when Brahmāṇī sprinkled them from her kamaṇḍalu
  • हतौजसः hataujasaḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: हत + ओजस् · Vigraha: हतम् ओजः येषां ते, तान् hatam ojaḥ yeṣāṃ te, tān
    Literally “those whose vigour is struck down”; in the verse: drained of energy

C. Morphology

  • अकरोत् akarot — Imperfect of √कृ (stem करो-, augment अ-). With two accusatives — the object शत्रून् and the predicate हतवीर्यान् हतौजसः — it means ‘made (them) such-and-such’.
  • धावति dhāvati — Present with स्म: a present tense used for a past action, a regular Sanskrit idiom (the particle स्म turns the present into the past). येन येन स्म धावति: ‘wherever she ran’.
  • हतौजसः hataujasaḥ — Accusative plural of the s-stem adjective हतौजस्; for s-stems the accusative plural has the same form as the nominative plural. It agrees with शत्रून्.

D. Anvaya — prose order

ब्रह्माणी च येन येन धावति स्म, (तत्र) शत्रून् कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपहतवीर्यान् हतौजसः अकरोत्।

brahmāṇī ca yena yena dhāvati sma, (tatra) śatrūn kamaṇḍalujalākṣepahatavīryān hataujasaḥ akarot |

Main clause ब्रह्माणी … शत्रून् … अकरोत् with two predicate accusatives; the relative clause येन येन धावति स्म says where.

6Translation

LiteralAnd Brahmāṇī, wherever she ran, made the enemies bereft of valour by the sprinkling of water from her water-pot, bereft of vigour.

ReadableWherever Brahmāṇī moved, she sprinkled the enemies with water from her water-pot and left them drained of valour and strength.

The compound’s instrument (the sprinkled water) is turned into an action of Brahmāṇī; the sense is unchanged.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe Mothers now fight, each with the emblem of the god she came from, as 8.14 announced. Brahmāṇī arrived carrying a rosary and a water-pot (8.15 साक्षसूत्रकमण्डलुः); her weapon is that water-pot. Her water does not wound; it takes the enemy’s strength away.

Traditional interpretationThe hymn of chapter 11 remembers the same act: Brahmāṇī is praised there as the one who sprinkles water with kuśa grass (11.13 कौशाम्भःक्षरिके). The rite-implement of the creator becomes a weapon in the Goddess’s hand.

8.34Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

माहेश्वरी त्रिशूलेन तथा चक्रेण वैष्णवी।
दैत्याञ्जघान कौमारी तथा शक्त्यातिकोपना ॥ ३४ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

माहेश्वरी | त्रिशूलेन | तथा | चक्रेण | वैष्णवीदैत्यान् | जघान | कौमारी | तथा | शक्त्या | अतिकोपना

3IAST

As recitedmāheśvarī triśūlena tathā cakreṇa vaiṣṇavī |
daityāñjaghāna kaumārī tathā śaktyātikopanā ||

Separatedmāheśvarī | triśūlena | tathā | cakreṇa | vaiṣṇavī
daityān | jaghāna | kaumārī | tathā | śaktyā | atikopanā

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
माहेश्वरीmāheśvarīMāheśvarī, the power of Maheśvara (Śiva)f. nom. sg. of माहेश्वरी (taddhita from महेश्वर, with feminine ई); a subject of जघान
त्रिशूलेनtriśūlenawith the tridentn. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument
तथाtathālikewise, andindeclinable (adverb of manner, here connective)
चक्रेणcakreṇawith the discusn. instr. sg. of चक्र; instrument
वैष्णवीvaiṣṇavīVaiṣṇavī, the power of Viṣṇuf. nom. sg. of वैष्णवी (taddhita from विष्णु); a subject of जघान
दैत्यान्daityānthe Daityasm. acc. pl. of दैत्य; object of जघान
जघानjaghānastruck down, slew√हन् (adādi, 2P ‘to strike, kill’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; construed with each of the three subjects in turn
कौमारीkaumārīKaumārī, the power of Kumāra (Skanda)f. nom. sg. of कौमारी (taddhita from कुमार); a subject of जघान
तथाtathālikewiseindeclinable (connective)
शक्त्याśaktyāwith the spear (śakti)f. instr. sg. of शक्ति (i-stem); here the weapon, not the goddess
अतिकोपनाatikopanāexceedingly wrathfulf. nom. sg. of अतिकोपन; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies कौमारी

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • दैत्याञ्जघान daityāñjaghāna → दैत्यान् + जघान
    consonant sandhi (n + j) · स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)
    A dental n before the palatal j becomes the palatal nasal ñ.
  • शक्त्यातिकोपना śaktyātikopanā → शक्त्या + अतिकोपना
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • त्रिशूलेन triśūlena — bahuvrīhi
    Members: त्रि + शूल · Vigraha: त्रीणि शूलानि यस्मिन् तत् त्रिशूलम्, तेन trīṇi śūlāni yasmin tat triśūlam, tena
    Literally “that which has three points”; in the verse: the trident, Śiva’s weapon
    Alternative: Also analysed as a samāhāra dvigu (त्रयाणां शूलानां समाहारः), ‘a set of three points’; the bahuvrīhi suits a single weapon better.
  • अतिकोपना atikopanā — prādi tatpuruṣa (karmadhāraya with the prefix अति)
    Members: अति + कोपना · Vigraha: अतिशयेन कोपना atiśayena kopanā
    Literally “excessively irascible”; in the verse: blazing with anger

C. Morphology

  • जघान jaghāna — Perfect of √हन्: reduplication ज-घान (h becomes gh in the root syllable and j in the reduplication). One singular verb serves three singular subjects, each with her own weapon: ‘Māheśvarī struck … Vaiṣṇavī … Kaumārī …’.
  • अतिकोपना atikopanā — कोपन ‘irascible’ is √कुप् with the agent affix -ana; feminine कोपना. With अति it is an intensive adjective.

D. Anvaya — prose order

माहेश्वरी त्रिशूलेन, तथा वैष्णवी चक्रेण, तथा अतिकोपना कौमारी शक्त्या दैत्यान् जघान।

māheśvarī triśūlena, tathā vaiṣṇavī cakreṇa, tathā atikopanā kaumārī śaktyā daityān jaghāna |

Three subject–instrument pairs share one verb and one object.

6Translation

LiteralMāheśvarī with the trident, likewise Vaiṣṇavī with the discus, and likewise Kaumārī, exceedingly wrathful, with the spear, struck down the Daityas.

ReadableMāheśvarī struck the Daityas down with her trident, Vaiṣṇavī with her discus, and Kaumārī, blazing with anger, with her spear.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThree more Mothers fight, each with her god’s weapon: Śiva’s trident, Viṣṇu’s discus, Skanda’s spear (the śakti Kaumārī held in 8.17, शक्तिहस्ता).

Traditional interpretationThe word शक्ति is used here in its weapon sense — the spear of Skanda — beside its other sense, ‘power’, in which the Mothers themselves are the gods’ śaktis (8.13). The text keeps both meanings in play.

8.35Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेन शतशो दैत्यदानवाः।
पेतुर्विदारिताः पृथ्व्यां रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः ॥ ३५ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेन | शतशः | दैत्यदानवाःपेतुः | विदारिताः | पृथ्व्याम् | रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः

3IAST

As recitedaindrīkuliśapātena śataśo daityadānavāḥ |
peturvidāritāḥ pṛthvyāṃ rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ ||

Separatedaindrīkuliśapātena | śataśaḥ | daityadānavāḥ
petuḥ | vidāritāḥ | pṛthvyām | rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेनaindrīkuliśapātenaby the fall of Aindrī’s thunderboltm. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument with विदारिताः
शतशःśataśaḥby hundredsindeclinable (taddhita -शस्, distributive)
दैत्यदानवाःdaityadānavāḥDaityas and Dānavasm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of पेतुः
पेतुःpetuḥfell√पत् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to fall’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
विदारिताःvidāritāḥtorn apartवि + √दॄ (causative दारि- ‘to tear’) + क्त (past passive participle), m. nom. pl.; qualifies दैत्यदानवाः
पृथ्व्याम्pṛthvyāmon the earthf. loc. sg. of पृथ्वी
रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणःrudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥpouring out torrents of bloodm. nom. pl. of रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिन्; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies दैत्यदानवाः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • शतशो दैत्य śataśo daitya → शतशः + दैत्य…
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    -aḥ before a voiced consonant (here d) becomes -o.
  • पेतुर्विदारिताः peturvidāritāḥ → पेतुः + विदारिताः
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    After a vowel other than a/ā, the visarga is sounded r before a voiced consonant.
  • रुधिरौघ rudhiraugha → रुधिर + ओघ
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    a + o → au.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेन aindrīkuliśapātena — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: ऐन्द्री + कुलिश + पात · Vigraha: ऐन्द्र्याः कुलिशम् ऐन्द्रीकुलिशम्; तस्य पातः, तेन aindryāḥ kuliśam aindrīkuliśam; tasya pātaḥ, tena
    Literally “by the falling of Aindrī’s thunderbolt”; in the verse: under the strokes of Aindrī’s vajra
  • दैत्यदानवाः daityadānavāḥ — itaretara dvandva
    Members: दैत्य + दानव · Vigraha: दैत्याश्च दानवाश्च daityāśca dānavāśca
    Literally “Daityas and Dānavas”; in the verse: the asura hosts of both clans (sons of Diti and of Danu)
  • रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ — upapada tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (रुधिरौघ)
    Members: रुधिर + ओघ + प्रवर्षिन् · Vigraha: रुधिरस्य ओघः रुधिरौघः; रुधिरौघं प्रवर्षन्ति इति रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः rudhirasya oghaḥ rudhiraughaḥ; rudhiraughaṃ pravarṣanti iti rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ
    Literally “raining floods of blood”; in the verse: streaming with blood as they fell

C. Morphology

  • पेतुः petuḥ — Perfect of √पत् formed without a reduplicated syllable: the root vowel becomes e (पेत्-), as in पेतुः ‘they fell’, नेशुः ‘they vanished’ (8.39).
  • विदारिताः vidāritāḥ — Past passive participle of the causative वि + √दॄ (दारय-): ‘made to burst, torn open’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेन विदारिताः रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः दैत्यदानवाः शतशः पृथ्व्यां पेतुः।

aindrīkuliśapātena vidāritāḥ rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ daityadānavāḥ śataśaḥ pṛthvyāṃ petuḥ |

6Translation

LiteralBy the fall of Aindrī’s thunderbolt Daityas and Dānavas, torn apart, fell by hundreds on the earth, raining torrents of blood.

ReadableTorn apart by the strokes of Aindrī’s thunderbolt, Daityas and Dānavas fell to the earth in their hundreds, pouring out streams of blood.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningAindrī, Indra’s power, who came holding the thunderbolt and riding the king of elephants (8.21), strikes with the vajra. The streams of blood prepare the next episode: Raktabīja, whose every drop of blood will become an asura.

Traditional interpretationThe chapter 11 hymn praises Aindrī as the one with the great thunderbolt who took the life of Vṛtra (11.19 महावज्रे … वृत्रप्राणहरे). Her weapon is Indra’s, and so is her history.

8.36Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ता दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसः।
वाराहमूर्त्या न्यपतंश्चक्रेण च विदारिताः ॥ ३६ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ताः | दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसःवाराहमूर्त्या | न्यपतन् | चक्रेण | च | विदारिताः

3IAST

As recitedtuṇḍaprahāravidhvastā daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥ |
vārāhamūrtyā nyapataṃścakreṇa ca vidāritāḥ ||

Separatedtuṇḍaprahāravidhvastāḥ | daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥ
vārāhamūrtyā | nyapatan | cakreṇa | ca | vidāritāḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ताःtuṇḍaprahāravidhvastāḥshattered by blows of the snoutm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies the understood subject (the asuras)
दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसःdaṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥtheir chests wounded by the tips of the tusksm. nom. pl. of the bahuvrīhi stem दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षस्; compound (see Samāsa)
वाराहमूर्त्याvārāhamūrtyāby the one in boar form, Vārāhīf. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); agent of the participles
न्यपतन्nyapatanfell downनि + √पत् (bhvādi, 1P), laṅ (imperfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
चक्रेणcakreṇaby the discusn. instr. sg. of चक्र; instrument with विदारिताः
चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
विदारिताःvidāritāḥtorn apartवि + √दॄ (causative) + क्त, m. nom. pl.

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • विध्वस्ता दंष्ट्रा vidhvastā daṃṣṭrā → विध्वस्ताः + दंष्ट्रा…
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)
    -āḥ before a voiced consonant loses its visarga.
  • न्यपतंश्चक्रेण nyapataṃścakreṇa → न्यपतन् + चक्रेण
    consonant sandhi (n + c) · नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)
    Final n before c becomes anusvāra + ś: न्यपतन् चक्रेण → न्यपतंश्चक्रेण.
  • न्यपतन् nyapatan → नि + अपतन्
    vowel sandhi (prefix + augmented verb) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    The i of नि becomes y before the augment अ-.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ताः tuṇḍaprahāravidhvastāḥ — tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (तुण्डप्रहार)
    Members: तुण्ड + प्रहार + विध्वस्त · Vigraha: तुण्डस्य प्रहारः तुण्डप्रहारः; तेन विध्वस्ताः tuṇḍasya prahāraḥ tuṇḍaprahāraḥ; tena vidhvastāḥ
    Literally “destroyed by the blow of the snout”; in the verse: smashed by the boar’s snout
  • दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसः daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥ — bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (दंष्ट्राग्र) and a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (…क्षत)
    Members: दंष्ट्रा + अग्र + क्षत + वक्षस् · Vigraha: दंष्ट्रायाः अग्रं दंष्ट्राग्रम्; दंष्ट्राग्रेण क्षतं वक्षः येषां ते daṃṣṭrāyāḥ agraṃ daṃṣṭrāgram; daṃṣṭrāgreṇa kṣataṃ vakṣaḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally “those whose chests are wounded by the tusk-tip”; in the verse: gored in the chest
  • वाराहमूर्त्या vārāhamūrtyā — karmadhāraya (the feminine adjective takes its masculine form as first member)
    Members: वाराह + मूर्ति · Vigraha: वाराही मूर्तिः वाराहमूर्तिः, तया vārāhī mūrtiḥ vārāhamūrtiḥ, tayā
    Literally “by the boar form”; in the verse: by Vārāhī, the power of the Boar
    Alternative: Or bahuvrīhi: वाराही मूर्तिः यस्याः सा, तया — ‘by her whose form is the boar’s’. Either way the agent is Vārāhī.

C. Morphology

  • न्यपतन् nyapatan — Imperfect of नि + √पत्: the augment अ- stands between the prefix and the root (नि-अ-पतन्), so the prefix’s i turns to y.
  • दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसः daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥ — A bahuvrīhi ending in the s-stem वक्षस् ‘chest’: nominative plural -वक्षसः, ‘(those) whose chests …’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

वाराहमूर्त्या तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ताः दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसः चक्रेण च विदारिताः (असुराः) न्यपतन्।

vārāhamūrtyā tuṇḍaprahāravidhvastāḥ daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥ cakreṇa ca vidāritāḥ (asurāḥ) nyapatan |

The subject (the asuras) is understood; three descriptions in the nominative plural, all with the agent वाराहमूर्त्या, lead to the verb न्यपतन्.

6Translation

LiteralShattered by blows of the snout, their chests wounded by the tips of the tusks, and torn apart by the discus, by the one in boar form, (the asuras) fell.

ReadableShattered by the blows of her snout, their chests gored by the tips of her tusks, torn apart by her discus, the asuras fell before Vārāhī.

‘Her’ is supplied: all three weapons are taken as Vārāhī’s (see Commentary).

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningVārāhī, who came in the form of the sacrificial Boar (8.19), fights as a boar: snout and tusks. The discus in the same sentence is most naturally hers too, though Vaiṣṇavī has just used a discus in 8.34.

Traditional interpretationThe chapter 11 hymn gives the boar-formed Goddess a great discus and the tusk that lifted the earth (11.17 गृहीतोग्रमहाचक्रे दंष्ट्रोद्धृतवसुंधरे), which supports reading the discus here as Vārāhī’s.

8.37Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

नखैर्विदारितांश्चान्यान् भक्षयन्ती महासुरान्।
नारसिंही चचाराजौ नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा ॥ ३७ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

नखैः | विदारितान् | च | अन्यान् | भक्षयन्ती | महासुरान्नारसिंही | चचार | आजौ | नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा

3IAST

As recitednakhairvidāritāṃścānyān bhakṣayantī mahāsurān |
nārasiṃhī cacārājau nādāpūrṇadigambarā ||

Separatednakhaiḥ | vidāritān | ca | anyān | bhakṣayantī | mahāsurān
nārasiṃhī | cacāra | ājau | nādāpūrṇadigambarā

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
नखैःnakhaiḥwith (her) clawsm. instr. pl. of नख; instrument with विदारितान्
विदारितान्vidāritāntorn apartवि + √दॄ (causative) + क्त, m. acc. pl.; qualifies महासुरान्
चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle; links this to the preceding verse
अन्यान्anyānotherpronominal adjective अन्य, m. acc. pl.; qualifies महासुरान्
भक्षयन्तीbhakṣayantīdevouring√भक्ष् (curādi, 10 ‘to eat’) + शतृ (present participle), f. nom. sg.; qualifies नारसिंही
महासुरान्mahāsurāngreat asurasm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); object of भक्षयन्ती
नारसिंहीnārasiṃhīNārasiṃhī, the power of the Man-Lionf. nom. sg. of नारसिंही (taddhita from नरसिंह); subject
चचारcacāraroamed, moved about√चर् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to move’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
आजौājauin the battlem. loc. sg. of आजि (i-stem)
नादापूर्णदिगम्बराnādāpūrṇadigambarāshe who filled the quarters and the sky with her roarf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies नारसिंही

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • नखैर्विदारितांश्चान्यान् nakhairvidāritāṃścānyān → नखैः + विदारितान् + च + अन्यान्
    visarga sandhi; consonant sandhi (n + c); vowel sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66); नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    नखैः before v is sounded नखैर्; विदारितान् before च becomes विदारितांश्; च + अन्यान् → चान्यान्.
  • चचाराजौ cacārājau → चचार + आजौ
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā.
  • नादापूर्ण nādāpūrṇa → नाद + आपूर्ण
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā. Further on, दिक् + अम्बर → दिगम्बर: a final stop is voiced before a vowel (झलां जशोऽन्ते, 8.2.39).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • महासुरान् mahāsurān — karmadhāraya
    Members: महत् + असुर · Vigraha: महान्तः असुराः, तान् mahāntaḥ asurāḥ, tān
    Literally “great asuras”; in the verse: the mightiest of the demons
  • नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा nādāpūrṇadigambarā — bahuvrīhi containing a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (नादापूर्ण) and a samāhāra dvandva (दिगम्बर)
    Members: नाद + आपूर्ण + दिश् + अम्बर · Vigraha: दिशश्च अम्बरं च दिगम्बरम्; नादेन आपूर्णं दिगम्बरं यया सा diśaśca ambaraṃ ca digambaram; nādena āpūrṇaṃ digambaraṃ yayā sā
    Literally “she by whom the quarters and sky are filled with roaring”; in the verse: her roars filled every direction and the heavens

C. Morphology

  • भक्षयन्ती bhakṣayantī — Present participle of √भक्ष् (stem भक्षय-), feminine in -ई with the nasal: भक्षयन्ती ‘devouring’. It governs the accusative महासुरान्.
  • चचार cacāra — Perfect of √चर्, reduplicated च-चार: ‘she moved about’.
  • आजौ ājau — Locative singular of the i-stem आजि ‘contest, battle’: -इ + locative → -औ.
  • नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा nādāpūrṇadigambarā — Read as one bahuvrīhi: ‘(she) by whom quarters-and-sky were filled with roaring’. Its last members, taken alone, would spell दिगम्बरा ‘sky-clad’, an epithet used elsewhere of Kālī; that is not the sense here.

D. Anvaya — prose order

नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा नारसिंही च नखैः विदारितान् अन्यान् महासुरान् भक्षयन्ती आजौ चचार।

nādāpūrṇadigambarā nārasiṃhī ca nakhaiḥ vidāritān anyān mahāsurān bhakṣayantī ājau cacāra |

Subject नारसिंही with her epithet; the participle phrase भक्षयन्ती with its object; the verb चचार with its place आजौ.

6Translation

LiteralAnd devouring other great asuras torn apart by her claws, Nārasiṃhī, who filled the quarters and the sky with her roar, roamed in the battle.

ReadableNārasiṃhī ranged over the field, filling the quarters and the sky with her roar, tearing other great asuras with her claws and devouring them.

The participle विदारितान् (‘torn’) is rendered as a second action of Nārasiṃhī; the Sanskrit makes the asuras the object of one verb, भक्षयन्ती.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningNārasiṃhī, who came in a body like the Man-Lion’s, scattering the stars with a toss of her mane (8.20), fights as a lion fights: with claws, teeth and roar. ‘Other’ (अन्यान्) sets her victims apart from those Vārāhī has just struck.

Traditional interpretationThe chapter 11 hymn remembers her as the Goddess in the fierce Man-Lion form who set herself to kill the Daityas (11.18 नृसिंहरूपेणोग्रेण हन्तुं दैत्यान् कृतोद्यमे). Here, as in the hymn, she is named only by her form.

Textual Verification Required / uncertain: अन्यान् can also be read apart from विदारितान् — ‘(those) torn by her claws, and others too’, both devoured. The sense differs little; the reading of the text is not in doubt.

8.38Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

चण्डाट्टहासैरसुराः शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः।
पेतुः पृथिव्यां पतितांस्तांश्चखादाथ सा तदा ॥ ३८ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

चण्डाट्टहासैः | असुराः | शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताःपेतुः | पृथिव्याम् | पतितान् | तान् | चखाद | अथ | सा | तदा

3IAST

As recitedcaṇḍāṭṭahāsairasurāḥ śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ |
petuḥ pṛthivyāṃ patitāṃstāṃścakhādātha sā tadā ||

Separatedcaṇḍāṭṭahāsaiḥ | asurāḥ | śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ
petuḥ | pṛthivyām | patitān | tān | cakhāda | atha | sā | tadā

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
चण्डाट्टहासैःcaṇḍāṭṭahāsaiḥby (her) fierce loud laughsm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument with अभिदूषिताः
असुराःasurāḥthe asurasm. nom. pl. of असुर; subject of पेतुः
शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताःśivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥstricken by Śivadūtīm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies असुराः
पेतुःpetuḥfell√पत् (bhvādi, 1P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
पृथिव्याम्pṛthivyāmon the earthf. loc. sg. of पृथिवी
पतितान्patitānfallen√पत् + क्त (past participle), m. acc. pl.; qualifies तान्
तान्tānthempronoun तद्, m. acc. pl.; object of चखाद
चखादcakhādadevoured√खाद् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to eat’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
अथathathenindeclinable (particle of sequence)
साsāshe (Śivadūtī)pronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of चखाद
तदाtadāat that timeindeclinable (adverb of time)

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • चण्डाट्टहासैरसुराः caṇḍāṭṭahāsairasurāḥ → चण्डाट्टहासैः + असुराः
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    After ai the visarga is sounded r before a vowel.
  • शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ → शिवदूती + अभिदूषिताः
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    ī becomes y before a.
  • पतितांस्तांश्चखादाथ patitāṃstāṃścakhādātha → पतितान् + तान् + चखाद + अथ
    consonant sandhi (n + t, n + c); vowel sandhi · नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7); स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    Final n becomes anusvāra + s before t (पतितांस्तान्) and anusvāra + ś before c (तांश्चखाद); चखाद + अथ → चखादाथ.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • चण्डाट्टहासैः caṇḍāṭṭahāsaiḥ — karmadhāraya (अट्टहास ‘loud laughter’ is itself a karmadhāraya: अट्टः हासः)
    Members: चण्ड + अट्टहास · Vigraha: चण्डाः अट्टहासाः, तैः caṇḍāḥ aṭṭahāsāḥ, taiḥ
    Literally “by fierce loud laughs”; in the verse: by her terrifying peals of laughter
  • शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ — tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa
    Members: शिवदूती + अभिदूषित · Vigraha: शिवदूत्या अभिदूषिताः śivadūtyā abhidūṣitāḥ
    Literally “assailed by Śivadūtī”; in the verse: struck down by Śivadūtī’s laughter
    Alternative: शिवदूती could be a separate nominative (‘Śivadūtī …’), but then अभिदूषिताः has no agent and सा in the second line repeats her; the compound fits better.

C. Morphology

  • चखाद cakhāda — Perfect of √खाद्, reduplicated च-खाद (kh → c in the reduplication).
  • शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ — अभिदूषित: अभि + causative of √दुष् (दूषय-) + क्त, ‘spoiled, harmed, assailed’. Here: overwhelmed by the laughter.

D. Anvaya — prose order

शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः असुराः चण्डाट्टहासैः पृथिव्यां पेतुः; अथ तदा सा पतितान् तान् चखाद।

śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ asurāḥ caṇḍāṭṭahāsaiḥ pṛthivyāṃ petuḥ; atha tadā sā patitān tān cakhāda |

Two clauses: the asuras fell; then she devoured them.

6Translation

LiteralThe asuras, assailed by Śivadūtī with fierce loud laughs, fell on the earth; and then she at that time devoured them as they lay fallen.

ReadableStruck down by Śivadūtī’s fierce peals of laughter, the asuras fell to the ground, and there she devoured them.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningŚivadūtī is the power that came out of Caṇḍikā’s own body with the cry of a hundred jackals (8.23) and sent Śiva as her messenger (8.28). Her weapon is her laughter: it fells the asuras, and she eats the fallen.

Traditional interpretationThe same terrible laughter returns in the next battle (9.23 अट्टाट्टहासमशिवं शिवदूती चकार ह), and the hymn of chapter 11 salutes her as Śivadūtī who slew the mighty Daityas (11.20).

8.39Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

इति मातृगणं क्रुद्धं मर्दयन्तं महासुरान्।
दृष्ट्वाभ्युपायैर्विविधैर्नेशुर्देवारिसैनिकाः ॥ ३९ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

इति | मातृगणम् | क्रुद्धम् | मर्दयन्तम् | महासुरान्दृष्ट्वा | अभ्युपायैः | विविधैः | नेशुः | देवारिसैनिकाः

3IAST

As recitediti mātṛgaṇaṃ kruddhaṃ mardayantaṃ mahāsurān |
dṛṣṭvābhyupāyairvividhairneśurdevārisainikāḥ ||

Separatediti | mātṛgaṇam | kruddham | mardayantam | mahāsurān
dṛṣṭvā | abhyupāyaiḥ | vividhaiḥ | neśuḥ | devārisainikāḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
इतिitithusindeclinable; sums up the scenes just described
मातृगणम्mātṛgaṇamthe band of Mothersm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of दृष्ट्वा
क्रुद्धम्kruddhamenraged√क्रुध् (divādi, 4P ‘to be angry’) + क्त, m. acc. sg.; qualifies मातृगणम्
मर्दयन्तम्mardayantamcrushing√मृद् (causative stem मर्दय- ‘to crush’) + शतृ (present participle), m. acc. sg.; qualifies मातृगणम्
महासुरान्mahāsurānthe great asurasm. acc. pl.; compound (karmadhāraya, as in 8.37); object of मर्दयन्तम्
दृष्ट्वाdṛṣṭvāhaving seen√दृश् (bhvādi, 1P) + क्त्वा (absolutive)
अभ्युपायैःabhyupāyaiḥby (various) means, in (various) waysm. instr. pl. of अभ्युपाय (अभि + उप + √इ ‘approach, expedient’)
विविधैःvividhaiḥvariousm. instr. pl. of विविध; qualifies अभ्युपायैः
नेशुःneśuḥvanished, fled√नश् (divādi, 4P ‘to be lost, disappear, perish’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
देवारिसैनिकाःdevārisainikāḥthe soldiers of the gods’ enemiesm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of नेशुः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • दृष्ट्वाभ्युपायैर्विविधैर्नेशुर्देवारि dṛṣṭvābhyupāyairvividhairneśurdevāri → दृष्ट्वा + अभ्युपायैः + विविधैः + नेशुः + देवारि…
    vowel sandhi; visarga sandhi · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    ā + a → ā; then three visargas after ai, ai and u are each sounded r before a voiced consonant (v, n, d).
  • देवारि devāri → देव + अरि
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • मातृगणम् mātṛgaṇam — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: मातृ + गण · Vigraha: मातॄणां गणः, तम् mātṝṇāṃ gaṇaḥ, tam
    Literally “the host of the Mothers”; in the verse: the Mātṛkās fighting together
  • देवारिसैनिकाः devārisainikāḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (देवारि)
    Members: देव + अरि + सैनिक · Vigraha: देवानाम् अरयः देवारयः; देवारीणां सैनिकाः devānām arayaḥ devārayaḥ; devārīṇāṃ sainikāḥ
    Literally “the soldiers of the enemies of the gods”; in the verse: the rank and file of the asura army

C. Morphology

  • नेशुः neśuḥ — Perfect of √नश्, formed like पेतुः (8.35) with e and no reduplicated syllable. √नश् means both ‘perish’ and ‘be lost, vanish’; with 8.40 (‘seeing the Daityas intent on flight’) the sense is ‘they made off, disappeared’.
  • मर्दयन्तम् mardayantam — Present participle of the causative stem मर्दय- of √मृद् ‘crush’, accusative singular agreeing with the collective मातृगणम्.

D. Anvaya — prose order

इति क्रुद्धं महासुरान् मर्दयन्तं मातृगणं दृष्ट्वा देवारिसैनिकाः विविधैः अभ्युपायैः नेशुः।

iti kruddhaṃ mahāsurān mardayantaṃ mātṛgaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā devārisainikāḥ vividhaiḥ abhyupāyaiḥ neśuḥ |

6Translation

LiteralSeeing thus the band of Mothers, enraged, crushing the great asuras, the soldiers of the gods’ enemies vanished by various means.

ReadableWhen they saw the furious band of Mothers crushing the great asuras, the soldiers of the gods’ enemies slipped away, each however he could.

नेशुः is taken as ‘vanished, fled’ (see morph); ‘each however he could’ renders अभ्युपायैर्विविधैः.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe rout of the common soldiers. The word for the Mothers is now collective — मातृगण, ‘the band of Mothers’ — and the asura rank and file disappear, as they will again in 9.41 (केचिद्विनेशुरसुराः केचिन्नष्टा महाहवात्).

Textual Verification Required / uncertain: नेशुः may also be rendered ‘perished (in various ways)’. The next verse speaks of the Daityas as ‘intent on flight’, so ‘vanished, fled’ is preferred.

8.40Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

पलायनपरान् दृष्ट्वा दैत्यान् मातृगणार्दितान्।
योद्धुमभ्याययौ क्रुद्धो रक्तबीजो महासुरः ॥ ४० ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

पलायनपरान् | दृष्ट्वा | दैत्यान् | मातृगणार्दितान्योद्धुम् | अभ्याययौ | क्रुद्धः | रक्तबीजः | महासुरः

3IAST

As recitedpalāyanaparān dṛṣṭvā daityān mātṛgaṇārditān |
yoddhumabhyāyayau kruddho raktabījo mahāsuraḥ ||

Separatedpalāyanaparān | dṛṣṭvā | daityān | mātṛgaṇārditān
yoddhum | abhyāyayau | kruddhaḥ | raktabījaḥ | mahāsuraḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
पलायनपरान्palāyanaparānintent on flightm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies दैत्यान्
दृष्ट्वाdṛṣṭvāhaving seen√दृश् + क्त्वा (absolutive)
दैत्यान्daityānthe Daityasm. acc. pl. of दैत्य; object of दृष्ट्वा
मातृगणार्दितान्mātṛgaṇārditānharried by the band of Mothersm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies दैत्यान्
योद्धुम्yoddhumto fight√युध् (divādi, 4Ā ‘to fight’) + तुमुन् (infinitive of purpose)
अभ्याययौabhyāyayaucame forwardअभि + आ + √या (adādi, 2P ‘to go’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
क्रुद्धःkruddhaḥenraged√क्रुध् + क्त, m. nom. sg.; qualifies रक्तबीजः
रक्तबीजःraktabījaḥRaktabījam. nom. sg.; compound used as a proper name (see Samāsa); subject
महासुरःmahāsuraḥthe great asuram. nom. sg.; karmadhāraya (महान् असुरः); in apposition to रक्तबीजः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • योद्धुमभ्याययौ yoddhumabhyāyayau → योद्धुम् + अभ्याययौ
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) applies only before a consonant
    Before a vowel the final m stays m and is written joined to the next word.
  • क्रुद्धो रक्तबीजो महासुरः kruddho raktabījo mahāsuraḥ → क्रुद्धः + रक्तबीजः + महासुरः
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    -aḥ before the voiced r and m becomes -o, twice.
  • मातृगणार्दितान् mātṛgaṇārditān → मातृगण + अर्दितान्
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • पलायनपरान् palāyanaparān — bahuvrīhi
    Members: पलायन + पर · Vigraha: पलायनं परं (प्रधानं) येषां ते, तान् palāyanaṃ paraṃ (pradhānaṃ) yeṣāṃ te, tān
    Literally “those for whom flight is the chief thing”; in the verse: bent on running away
  • मातृगणार्दितान् mātṛgaṇārditān — tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (मातृगण as in 8.39)
    Members: मातृगण + अर्दित · Vigraha: मातृगणेन अर्दिताः, तान् mātṛgaṇena arditāḥ, tān
    Literally “afflicted by the band of Mothers”; in the verse: routed by the Mātṛkās
  • रक्तबीजः raktabījaḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: रक्त + बीज · Vigraha: रक्तं बीजं यस्य सः raktaṃ bījaṃ yasya saḥ
    Literally “he whose blood is seed”; in the verse: the asura from each drop of whose blood another rises (8.41)

C. Morphology

  • अभ्याययौ abhyāyayau — Perfect of √या (ययौ) with two prefixes: अभि + आ + ययौ → अभ्याययौ (अभि’s i becomes y before आ, इको यणचि 6.1.77). The same verb brought the Mothers to the field (8.17 अभ्याययौ).
  • योद्धुम् yoddhum — Infinitive of √युध्: युध् + तुम् → योद्धुम् (guṇa; dh + t → ddh).

D. Anvaya — prose order

मातृगणार्दितान् दैत्यान् पलायनपरान् दृष्ट्वा क्रुद्धः रक्तबीजः महासुरः योद्धुम् अभ्याययौ।

mātṛgaṇārditān daityān palāyanaparān dṛṣṭvā kruddhaḥ raktabījaḥ mahāsuraḥ yoddhum abhyāyayau |

6Translation

LiteralSeeing the Daityas, harried by the band of Mothers, intent on flight, the great asura Raktabīja, enraged, came forward to fight.

ReadableWhen he saw the Daityas, harried by the Mothers, turning to flee, the great asura Raktabīja advanced in fury to fight.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningRaktabīja enters, and the chapter takes its name from him (रक्तबीजवधः). His name is explained by the verse that follows: his blood is seed.

8.41Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

रक्तबिन्दुर्यदा भूमौ पतत्यस्य शरीरतः।
समुत्पतति मेदिन्यां तत्प्रमाणस्तदासुरः ॥ ४१ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

रक्तबिन्दुः | यदा | भूमौ | पतति | अस्य | शरीरतःसमुत्पतति | मेदिन्याम् | तत्प्रमाणः | तदा | असुरः

3IAST

As recitedraktabinduryadā bhūmau patatyasya śarīrataḥ |
samutpatati medinyāṃ tatpramāṇastadāsuraḥ ||

Separatedraktabinduḥ | yadā | bhūmau | patati | asya | śarīrataḥ
samutpatati | medinyām | tatpramāṇaḥ | tadā | asuraḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
रक्तबिन्दुःraktabinduḥa drop of bloodm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of पतति
यदाyadāwheneverindeclinable (relative adverb of time); correlative तदा
भूमौbhūmauon the groundf. loc. sg. of भूमि (i-stem)
पततिpatatifalls√पत् (bhvādi, 1P), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
अस्यasyahispronoun इदम्, m. gen. sg.; with शरीरतः
शरीरतःśarīrataḥfrom the bodyindeclinable: शरीर + taddhita -तस् (ablative sense)
समुत्पततिsamutpatatisprings upसम् + उद् + √पत्, laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
मेदिन्याम्medinyāmon the earthf. loc. sg. of मेदिनी
तत्प्रमाणःtatpramāṇaḥof his sizem. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies असुरः
तदाtadāthenindeclinable (correlative adverb of time)
असुरःasuraḥan asuram. nom. sg.; subject of समुत्पतति

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • रक्तबिन्दुर्यदा raktabinduryadā → रक्तबिन्दुः + यदा
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    After u the visarga is sounded r before the voiced y.
  • पतत्यस्य patatyasya → पतति + अस्य
    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    i becomes y before a.
  • तत्प्रमाणस्तदासुरः tatpramāṇastadāsuraḥ → तत्प्रमाणः + तदा + असुरः
    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    Visarga before t becomes s; तदा + असुरः → तदासुरः.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • रक्तबिन्दुः raktabinduḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: रक्त + बिन्दु · Vigraha: रक्तस्य बिन्दुः raktasya binduḥ
    Literally “a drop of blood”; in the verse: each single drop
  • तत्प्रमाणः tatpramāṇaḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: तद् + प्रमाण · Vigraha: तस्य प्रमाणम् इव प्रमाणं यस्य सः tasya pramāṇam iva pramāṇaṃ yasya saḥ
    Literally “he whose measure is (like) his”; in the verse: as big and as strong as Raktabīja himself

C. Morphology

  • पतति patati — Present tense for a standing fact: ‘whenever a drop falls, an asura springs up’. The narrative pauses to state the rule of his nature.
  • शरीरतः śarīrataḥ — The taddhita -तस् gives an indeclinable with ablative meaning: शरीरतः = शरीरात्, ‘from the body’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

यदा अस्य शरीरतः रक्तबिन्दुः भूमौ पतति, तदा मेदिन्यां तत्प्रमाणः असुरः समुत्पतति।

yadā asya śarīrataḥ raktabinduḥ bhūmau patati, tadā medinyāṃ tatpramāṇaḥ asuraḥ samutpatati |

A यदा … तदा sentence in the present: condition and consequence.

6Translation

LiteralWhenever a drop of blood falls from his body to the ground, then an asura of his size springs up on the earth.

ReadableWhenever a drop of his blood fell to the ground, an asura of his own stature sprang up from the earth.

The Sanskrit present of general truth is rendered as a past, to keep the narrative tense.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe verse states Raktabīja’s peculiar power: every drop of blood that reaches the earth becomes another asura his equal. The Devī Māhātmya gives the rule without explaining where it came from.

Modern reflectionA modern reading: violence that only spills more of what it attacks multiplies it. The episode is often read today as a picture of habits and desires that grow each time they are fought on their own terms.

Textual Verification Required / uncertain: Variant (corrections.json): for the Gita Press तत्प्रमाणस्तदासुरः, GRETIL and Freund read तत्प्रमाणो महासुरः (‘a great asura of his size’). The Gita Press reading is kept; the sense is the same, only तदा (the correlative of यदा) is replaced by महा-.

8.42Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

युयुधे स गदापाणिरिन्द्रशक्त्या महासुरः।
ततश्चैन्द्री स्ववज्रेण रक्तबीजमताडयत् ॥ ४२ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

युयुधे | सः | गदापाणिः | इन्द्रशक्त्या | महासुरःततः | च | ऐन्द्री | स्ववज्रेण | रक्तबीजम् | अताडयत्

3IAST

As recitedyuyudhe sa gadāpāṇirindraśaktyā mahāsuraḥ |
tataścaindrī svavajreṇa raktabījamatāḍayat ||

Separatedyuyudhe | saḥ | gadāpāṇiḥ | indraśaktyā | mahāsuraḥ
tataḥ | ca | aindrī | svavajreṇa | raktabījam | atāḍayat

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
युयुधेyuyudhefought√युध् (divādi, 4Ā ‘to fight’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada
सःsaḥhepronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.
गदापाणिःgadāpāṇiḥmace in handm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies सः
इन्द्रशक्त्याindraśaktyāwith Indra’s power (Aindrī)f. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrumental of association with युयुधे (‘fought with’)
महासुरःmahāsuraḥthe great asuram. nom. sg.; karmadhāraya (as in 8.40); in apposition to सः
ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb, -तस्)
चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
ऐन्द्रीaindrīAindrīf. nom. sg. of ऐन्द्री (taddhita from इन्द्र); subject
स्ववज्रेणsvavajreṇawith her own thunderboltm. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument
रक्तबीजम्raktabījamRaktabījam. acc. sg.; object of अताडयत्
अताडयत्atāḍayatstruck√तड् (curādi, 10 ‘to strike’, stem ताडय-), laṅ (imperfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • स गदापाणिरिन्द्र sa gadāpāṇirindra → सः + गदापाणिः + इन्द्र…
    visarga sandhi · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132); ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    सः drops its visarga before a consonant; गदापाणिः, after i, is sounded r before the vowel of इन्द्र.
  • ततश्चैन्द्री tataścaindrī → ततः + च + ऐन्द्री
    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    Visarga before c becomes ś; च + ऐन्द्री: a + ai → ai.
  • रक्तबीजमताडयत् raktabījamatāḍayat → रक्तबीजम् + अताडयत्
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) applies only before a consonant
    The m stays and is written joined.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • गदापाणिः gadāpāṇiḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: गदा + पाणि · Vigraha: गदा पाणौ यस्य सः gadā pāṇau yasya saḥ
    Literally “he in whose hand is a mace”; in the verse: armed with a mace
  • इन्द्रशक्त्या indraśaktyā — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: इन्द्र + शक्ति · Vigraha: इन्द्रस्य शक्तिः, तया indrasya śaktiḥ, tayā
    Literally “with the power of Indra”; in the verse: with Aindrī, named in the next line
  • स्ववज्रेण svavajreṇa — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: स्व + वज्र · Vigraha: स्वस्य वज्रः, तेन svasya vajraḥ, tena
    Literally “with the thunderbolt of her own”; in the verse: with her own vajra

C. Morphology

  • युयुधे yuyudhe — Perfect of √युध् in the ātmanepada, as the root requires: reduplicated यु-युध्-, ending -ए. Contrast the parasmaipada युयुधुः in 8.45.
  • अताडयत् atāḍayat — Imperfect of the curādi stem ताडय- (√तड्): augment अ-, ending -त्.

D. Anvaya — prose order

सः गदापाणिः महासुरः इन्द्रशक्त्या (सह) युयुधे; ततः च ऐन्द्री स्ववज्रेण रक्तबीजम् अताडयत्।

saḥ gadāpāṇiḥ mahāsuraḥ indraśaktyā (saha) yuyudhe; tataḥ ca aindrī svavajreṇa raktabījam atāḍayat |

6Translation

LiteralHe, the great asura, mace in hand, fought with the power of Indra; and then Aindrī struck Raktabīja with her own thunderbolt.

ReadableMace in hand, the great asura fought with Indra’s power, and Aindrī struck Raktabīja with her thunderbolt.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningRaktabīja’s first opponent is Aindrī. Her blow with the vajra draws the first blood, and the next verse shows what it produces.

8.43Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

कुलिशेनाहतस्याशु बहु सुस्राव शोणितम्।
समुत्तस्थुस्ततो योधास्तद्रूपास्तत्पराक्रमाः ॥ ४३ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

कुलिशेन | आहतस्य | आशु | बहु | सुस्राव | शोणितम्समुत्तस्थुः | ततः | योधाः | तद्रूपाः | तत्पराक्रमाः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • कुलिशेनाहतस्याशु kuliśenāhatasyāśu → कुलिशेन + आहतस्य + आशु
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā, twice.
  • समुत्तस्थुस्ततो योधास्तद्रूपास्तत्पराक्रमाः samuttasthustato yodhāstadrūpāstatparākramāḥ → समुत्तस्थुः + ततः + योधाः + तद्रूपाः + तत्पराक्रमाः
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    Visarga before t becomes s (three times); ततः before the voiced y becomes ततो.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • तद्रूपाः tadrūpāḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: तद् + रूप · Vigraha: तस्य रूपम् इव रूपं येषां ते tasya rūpam iva rūpaṃ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally “those whose form is (like) his”; in the verse: exact likenesses of Raktabīja
  • तत्पराक्रमाः tatparākramāḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: तद् + पराक्रम · Vigraha: तस्य पराक्रमः इव पराक्रमः येषां ते tasya parākramaḥ iva parākramaḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally “those whose valour is (like) his”; in the verse: as strong in battle as he

C. Morphology

  • सुस्राव susrāva — Perfect of √स्रु: reduplication सु-स्राव, with vṛddhi of the root vowel in the third singular.
  • समुत्तस्थुः samuttasthuḥ — Perfect of √स्था (तस्थुः ‘they stood’) with सम् + उद्: उद् + स्था loses the s of the root (उत्था-), so उत्-तस्थुः. ‘They rose up together’.
  • आहतस्य āhatasya — Genitive of the past participle, without a noun: ‘of (him) struck’. It names the source of the blood.

D. Anvaya — prose order

कुलिशेन आहतस्य (तस्य) आशु बहु शोणितं सुस्राव; ततः तद्रूपाः तत्पराक्रमाः योधाः समुत्तस्थुः।

kuliśena āhatasya (tasya) āśu bahu śoṇitaṃ susrāva; tataḥ tadrūpāḥ tatparākramāḥ yodhāḥ samuttasthuḥ |

6Translation

LiteralFrom him, struck by the thunderbolt, much blood flowed at once; from it rose up warriors of his form and his prowess.

ReadableStruck by the thunderbolt, he bled at once and freely, and from that blood rose warriors with his form and his strength.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe rule of 8.41 is now seen working: the first wound fills the field with Raktabīja’s doubles. ततः can be ‘then’ or ‘from it’; both fit, since the warriors rise from the blood.

8.44Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

यावन्तः पतितास्तस्य शरीराद्रक्तबिन्दवः।
तावन्तः पुरुषा जातास्तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाः ॥ ४४ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

यावन्तः | पतिताः | तस्य | शरीरात् | रक्तबिन्दवःतावन्तः | पुरुषाः | जाताः | तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाः

3IAST

As recitedyāvantaḥ patitāstasya śarīrādraktabindavaḥ |
tāvantaḥ puruṣā jātāstadvīryabalavikramāḥ ||

Separatedyāvantaḥ | patitāḥ | tasya | śarīrāt | raktabindavaḥ
tāvantaḥ | puruṣāḥ | jātāḥ | tadvīryabalavikramāḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
यावन्तःyāvantaḥas many asrelative quantitative यावत्, m. nom. pl.; correlative तावन्तः
पतिताःpatitāḥfell√पत् + क्त (past participle), m. nom. pl.; used as the predicate (finite past)
तस्यtasyahispronoun तद्, m. gen. sg.
शरीरात्śarīrātfrom the bodyn. abl. sg. of शरीर
रक्तबिन्दवःraktabindavaḥdrops of bloodm. nom. pl.; compound (ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, as in 8.41); subject
तावन्तःtāvantaḥso manycorrelative quantitative तावत्, m. nom. pl.
पुरुषाःpuruṣāḥmen, beingsm. nom. pl. of पुरुष; subject of जाताः
जाताःjātāḥwere born√जन् (divādi, 4Ā ‘to be born’) + क्त, m. nom. pl.; used as the predicate
तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाःtadvīryabalavikramāḥwith his valour, strength and prowessm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies पुरुषाः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • पतितास्तस्य patitāstasya → पतिताः + तस्य
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)
    Visarga before t becomes s; so also जातास्तद्वीर्य…
  • शरीराद्रक्तबिन्दवः śarīrādraktabindavaḥ → शरीरात् + रक्तबिन्दवः
    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)
    Final t becomes d before the voiced r.
  • पुरुषा जाता puruṣā jātā → पुरुषाः + जाताः
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)
    -āḥ loses its visarga before a voiced consonant.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाः tadvīryabalavikramāḥ — bahuvrīhi containing an itaretara dvandva (वीर्यबलविक्रम)
    Members: तद् + वीर्य + बल + विक्रम · Vigraha: वीर्यं च बलं च विक्रमः च वीर्यबलविक्रमाः; तस्य वीर्यबलविक्रमाः इव वीर्यबलविक्रमाः येषां ते vīryaṃ ca balaṃ ca vikramaḥ ca vīryabalavikramāḥ; tasya vīryabalavikramāḥ iva vīryabalavikramāḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally “those whose valour, strength and prowess are (like) his”; in the verse: his equals in every power

C. Morphology

  • यावन्तः yāvantaḥ — यावत् … तावत् ‘as many … so many’, declined like a participle in -vat: nominative plural यावन्तः, तावन्तः.
  • जाताः jātāḥ — Past participle of √जन् (जा-त) used as a finite verb, with अभवन् understood.

D. Anvaya — prose order

तस्य शरीरात् यावन्तः रक्तबिन्दवः पतिताः, तावन्तः तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाः पुरुषाः जाताः।

tasya śarīrāt yāvantaḥ raktabindavaḥ patitāḥ, tāvantaḥ tadvīryabalavikramāḥ puruṣāḥ jātāḥ |

A यावत् … तावत् sentence of quantity.

6Translation

LiteralAs many drops of blood as fell from his body, so many men were born with his valour, strength and prowess.

ReadableFor every drop of blood that fell from his body a warrior was born, with his valour, his strength and his prowess.

‘For every … a’ renders the correlatives ‘as many … so many’.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe narrator states the arithmetic of the danger: one drop, one Raktabīja. Each copy has not only his shape (8.43) but his full power.

8.45Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

ते चापि युयुधुस्तत्र पुरुषा रक्तसम्भवाः।
समं मातृभिरत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम् ॥ ४५ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

ते | च | अपि | युयुधुः | तत्र | पुरुषाः | रक्तसम्भवाःसमम् | मातृभिः | अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम्

3IAST

As recitedte cāpi yuyudhustatra puruṣā raktasambhavāḥ |
samaṃ mātṛbhiratyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇam ||

Separatedte | ca | api | yuyudhuḥ | tatra | puruṣāḥ | raktasambhavāḥ
samam | mātṛbhiḥ | atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇam

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
तेtetheypronoun तद्, m. nom. pl.
चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
अपिapitooindeclinable (particle of inclusion)
युयुधुःyuyudhuḥfought√युध् (divādi, 4Ā), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, in a parasmaipada form
तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place)
पुरुषाःpuruṣāḥmen, warriorsm. nom. pl. of पुरुष; in apposition to ते
रक्तसम्भवाःraktasambhavāḥborn of the bloodm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies पुरुषाः
समम्samamwith, together withindeclinable, governs the instrumental
मातृभिःmātṛbhiḥthe Mothersf. instr. pl. of मातृ (ṛ-stem); with समम्
अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम्atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇammost terribly, with the fall of very fierce weaponsn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); used adverbially (kriyāviśeṣaṇa) with युयुधुः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • चापि cāpi → च + अपि
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.
  • युयुधुस्तत्र yuyudhustatra → युयुधुः + तत्र
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)
    Visarga before t becomes s.
  • मातृभिरत्युग्र mātṛbhiratyugra → मातृभिः + अत्युग्र…
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    After i the visarga is sounded r before a vowel.
  • पातातिभीषणम् pātātibhīṣaṇam → पात + अतिभीषणम्
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā; likewise अति + उग्र → अत्युग्र by इको यणचि (6.1.77).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • रक्तसम्भवाः raktasambhavāḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: रक्त + सम्भव · Vigraha: रक्तात् सम्भवः येषां ते raktāt sambhavaḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally “those whose origin is from blood”; in the verse: born of Raktabīja’s blood
  • अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम् atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇam — tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa and a karmadhāraya (अत्युग्रशस्त्र, whose first member अत्युग्र is a prādi compound)
    Members: अति + उग्र + शस्त्र + पात + अति + भीषण · Vigraha: अत्युग्राणि शस्त्राणि अत्युग्रशस्त्राणि; तेषां पातः अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातः; तेन अतिभीषणम् atyugrāṇi śastrāṇi atyugraśastrāṇi; teṣāṃ pātaḥ atyugraśastrapātaḥ; tena atibhīṣaṇam
    Literally “exceedingly terrible through the falling of exceedingly fierce weapons”; in the verse: a fight made dreadful by a rain of savage blows
    Alternative: It may also qualify an understood cognate object युद्धम् (‘fought a battle most terrible …’); the meaning is the same.

C. Morphology

  • युयुधुः yuyudhuḥ — √युध् is an ātmanepada root (8.42 युयुधे); the classical third plural would be युयुधिरे. युयुधुः, with the parasmaipada ending, is an epic (ārṣa) form that the Purāṇas use freely.
  • अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम् atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇam — A long compound in the neuter accusative singular serving as an adverb of manner: ‘(fought) in a manner most terrible with …’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

ते रक्तसम्भवाः पुरुषाः च अपि तत्र मातृभिः समम् अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणं युयुधुः।

te raktasambhavāḥ puruṣāḥ ca api tatra mātṛbhiḥ samam atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇaṃ yuyudhuḥ |

6Translation

LiteralAnd those men too, born of the blood, fought there with the Mothers, most terribly with the fall of very fierce weapons.

ReadableThe warriors born of the blood fought the Mothers too, a battle made terrible by the crash of savage weapons.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe doubles do not merely appear; they fight, and the Mothers now face an army that grows with every wound they inflict.

8.46Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

पुनश्च वज्रपातेन क्षतमस्य शिरो यदा।
ववाह रक्तं पुरुषास्ततो जाताः सहस्रशः ॥ ४६ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

पुनः | च | वज्रपातेन | क्षतम् | अस्य | शिरः | यदाववाह | रक्तम् | पुरुषाः | ततः | जाताः | सहस्रशः

3IAST

As recitedpunaśca vajrapātena kṣatamasya śiro yadā |
vavāha raktaṃ puruṣāstato jātāḥ sahasraśaḥ ||

Separatedpunaḥ | ca | vajrapātena | kṣatam | asya | śiraḥ | yadā
vavāha | raktam | puruṣāḥ | tataḥ | jātāḥ | sahasraśaḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
पुनःpunaḥagainindeclinable (adverb; its visarga stands for r)
चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
वज्रपातेनvajrapātenaby the fall of the thunderboltm. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument with क्षतम्
क्षतम्kṣatamwounded√क्षण् (tanādi, 8U ‘to wound’) + क्त, n. nom. sg.; predicate of शिरः
अस्यasyahispronoun इदम्, m. gen. sg.
शिरःśiraḥheadn. nom. sg. of शिरस् (s-stem); subject
यदाyadāwhenindeclinable (relative adverb of time)
ववाहvavāhapoured out, streamed with√वह् (bhvādi, 1U ‘to carry, flow’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; subject शिरः
रक्तम्raktambloodn. acc. sg. of रक्त; object of ववाह
पुरुषाःpuruṣāḥmen, warriorsm. nom. pl. of पुरुष; subject of जाताः
ततःtataḥfrom thatindeclinable (-तस्)
जाताःjātāḥwere born√जन् + क्त, m. nom. pl.; used as the predicate
सहस्रशःsahasraśaḥby thousandsindeclinable (taddhita -शस्, distributive)

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • पुनश्च punaśca → पुनः + च
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)
    Visarga before c becomes ś.
  • क्षतमस्य शिरो यदा kṣatamasya śiro yadā → क्षतम् + अस्य + शिरः + यदा
    final m before a vowel; visarga sandhi · हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    क्षतम् stays m before the vowel; शिरः before the voiced y becomes शिरो.
  • पुरुषास्ततो जाताः puruṣāstato jātāḥ → पुरुषाः + ततः + जाताः
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); हशि च (6.1.114)
    पुरुषाः before t → पुरुषास्; ततः before the voiced j → ततो.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • वज्रपातेन vajrapātena — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: वज्र + पात · Vigraha: वज्रस्य पातः, तेन vajrasya pātaḥ, tena
    Literally “by the fall of the thunderbolt”; in the verse: by a stroke of Aindrī’s vajra

C. Morphology

  • ववाह vavāha — Perfect of √वह् with reduplication व-वाह. Used here of the wounded head ‘carrying’ blood, i.e. streaming with it.
  • क्षतम् kṣatam — Past participle of √क्षण् (the nasal is lost before -त): क्षत ‘wounded’, here a predicate with आसीत् understood.

D. Anvaya — prose order

पुनः च यदा वज्रपातेन अस्य शिरः क्षतम् (आसीत्), (तदा तत्) रक्तं ववाह; ततः सहस्रशः पुरुषाः जाताः।

punaḥ ca yadā vajrapātena asya śiraḥ kṣatam (āsīt), (tadā tat) raktaṃ vavāha; tataḥ sahasraśaḥ puruṣāḥ jātāḥ |

6Translation

LiteralAnd again, when his head was wounded by the fall of the thunderbolt, it poured out blood; from that men were born by thousands.

ReadableAgain the thunderbolt struck and wounded his head; the blood poured out, and from it warriors were born in their thousands.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningA second stroke of the vajra, to the head this time. The numbers rise from hundreds to thousands: each blow makes the problem worse.

8.47Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

वैष्णवी समरे चैनं चक्रेणाभिजघान ह।
गदया ताडयामास ऐन्द्री तमसुरेश्वरम् ॥ ४७ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

वैष्णवी | समरे | च | एनम् | चक्रेण | अभिजघान | हगदया | ताडयामास | ऐन्द्री | तम् | असुरेश्वरम्

3IAST

As recitedvaiṣṇavī samare cainaṃ cakreṇābhijaghāna ha |
gadayā tāḍayāmāsa aindrī tamasureśvaram ||

Separatedvaiṣṇavī | samare | ca | enam | cakreṇa | abhijaghāna | ha
gadayā | tāḍayāmāsa | aindrī | tam | asureśvaram

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
वैष्णवीvaiṣṇavīVaiṣṇavīf. nom. sg.; subject of अभिजघान
समरेsamarein the battlem. loc. sg. of समर
चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
एनम्enamhimpronoun एनद् (the unemphatic ‘him’ of renewed reference), m. acc. sg.; object
चक्रेणcakreṇawith the discusn. instr. sg. of चक्र; instrument
अभिजघानabhijaghānastruckअभि + √हन् (adādi, 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
हhaindeedindeclinable, emphatic particle (often a verse-filler)
गदयाgadayāwith a macef. instr. sg. of गदा; instrument
ताडयामासtāḍayāmāsastruck√तड् (curādi, 10, stem ताडय-), periphrastic liṭ (perfect, with आम् + आस), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
ऐन्द्रीaindrīAindrīf. nom. sg.; subject of ताडयामास
तम्tamthatpronoun तद्, m. acc. sg.; qualifies असुरेश्वरम्
असुरेश्वरम्asureśvaramlord of the asurasm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of ताडयामास

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • चैनं cainaṃ → च + एनम्
    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    a + e → ai. The final m of एनम् becomes anusvāra before the c of चक्रेण (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).
  • चक्रेणाभिजघान cakreṇābhijaghāna → चक्रेण + अभिजघान
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.
  • ताडयामास ऐन्द्री tāḍayāmāsa aindrī → ताडयामास + ऐन्द्री
    hiatus (no sandhi) at the pāda break · an exception to वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88): metrical licence
    Regular sandhi would give ताडयामासैन्द्री; the text keeps the words apart at the pāda boundary. Declared in the sandhi exceptions.
  • असुरेश्वरम् asureśvaram → असुर + ईश्वरम्
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    a + ī → e.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • असुरेश्वरम् asureśvaram — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: असुर + ईश्वर · Vigraha: असुराणाम् ईश्वरः, तम् asurāṇām īśvaraḥ, tam
    Literally “the lord of the asuras”; in the verse: Raktabīja, as a commander of the asura host

C. Morphology

  • ताडयामास tāḍayāmāsa — Periphrastic perfect: stem ताडय- + आम् + the perfect of √अस् (आस). Derived stems (causative, curādi) form their perfect this way.
  • एनम् enam — एनद् is used only in the accusative, instrumental singular and a few other forms, for a person already mentioned: ‘him’ (Raktabīja).

D. Anvaya — prose order

वैष्णवी च समरे एनं चक्रेण अभिजघान ह; ऐन्द्री तम् असुरेश्वरं गदया ताडयामास।

vaiṣṇavī ca samare enaṃ cakreṇa abhijaghāna ha; aindrī tam asureśvaraṃ gadayā tāḍayāmāsa |

Left unjoined: ताडयामास | ऐन्द्री — Hiatus at the pāda break (गदया ताडयामास is a full pāda of eight syllables): the a of ताडयामास is not fused with the ai of ऐन्द्री (which would give ताडयामासैन्द्री). A licence of epic verse, which may treat the pāda boundary as a pause; the text is printed so in the Gita Press.

6Translation

LiteralAnd Vaiṣṇavī indeed struck him in battle with the discus; Aindrī struck that lord of the asuras with a mace.

ReadableVaiṣṇavī struck him in battle with her discus, and Aindrī struck the asura lord with a mace.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningTwo Mothers attack together. The text gives Aindrī a mace (गदा) here, where earlier she used her thunderbolt (8.42, 8.46); the Gita Press reading is kept as it stands.

Textual Verification Required / uncertain: गदया with ऐन्द्री: Aindrī’s weapon elsewhere in the chapter is the vajra. corrections.json logs no variant for this verse; the mace is translated as written.

8.48Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैः।
सहस्रशो जगद्व्याप्तं तत्प्रमाणैर्महासुरैः ॥ ४८ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य | रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैःसहस्रशः | जगत् | व्याप्तम् | तत्प्रमाणैः | महासुरैः

3IAST

As recitedvaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya rudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥ |
sahasraśo jagadvyāptaṃ tatpramāṇairmahāsuraiḥ ||

Separatedvaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya | rudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥ
sahasraśaḥ | jagat | vyāptam | tatpramāṇaiḥ | mahāsuraiḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्यvaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasyaof him, split by Vaiṣṇavī’s discusm. gen. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); depends on the रुधिरस्राव- of the next word
रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैःrudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥborn from the flow of bloodm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies महासुरैः
सहस्रशःsahasraśaḥby thousandsindeclinable (taddhita -शस्)
जगत्jagatthe worldn. nom. sg. of जगत्; subject of the passive व्याप्तम्
व्याप्तम्vyāptamwas pervaded, filledवि + √आप् (svādi, 5P ‘to reach’) + क्त, n. nom. sg.; predicate (with आसीत् understood)
तत्प्रमाणैःtatpramāṇaiḥof his sizem. instr. pl.; bahuvrīhi (as in 8.41); qualifies महासुरैः
महासुरैःmahāsuraiḥby great asurasm. instr. pl.; karmadhāraya (महान्तः असुराः); agent of व्याप्तम्

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • सहस्रशो जगद्व्याप्तं sahasraśo jagadvyāptaṃ → सहस्रशः + जगत् + व्याप्तम्
    visarga sandhi; consonant sandhi (voicing) · हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87); झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)
    -aḥ before the voiced j becomes -o; the final t of जगत् becomes d before the voiced v.
  • तत्प्रमाणैर्महासुरैः tatpramāṇairmahāsuraiḥ → तत्प्रमाणैः + महासुरैः
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    After ai the visarga is sounded r before the voiced m.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य vaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya — tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (वैष्णवीचक्र)
    Members: वैष्णवी + चक्र + भिन्न · Vigraha: वैष्णव्याः चक्रं वैष्णवीचक्रम्; तेन भिन्नः, तस्य vaiṣṇavyāḥ cakraṃ vaiṣṇavīcakram; tena bhinnaḥ, tasya
    Literally “of the one split by Vaiṣṇavī’s discus”; in the verse: of Raktabīja, gashed by the discus of 8.47
  • रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैः rudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥ — bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (रुधिरस्राव)
    Members: रुधिर + स्राव + सम्भव · Vigraha: रुधिरस्य स्रावः रुधिरस्रावः; रुधिरस्रावात् सम्भवः येषां तैः rudhirasya srāvaḥ rudhirasrāvaḥ; rudhirasrāvāt sambhavaḥ yeṣāṃ taiḥ
    Literally “by those whose origin is from the flow of blood”; in the verse: born of the streaming blood

C. Morphology

  • व्याप्तम् vyāptam — Past passive participle of वि + √आप्: ‘reached through, pervaded’. With the instrumental agent महासुरैः it makes a passive sentence: ‘the world was filled by great asuras’.
  • वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य vaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya — भिन्न is the past participle of √भिद् ‘split’, with -न instead of -त. The genitive names whose blood it was.

D. Anvaya — prose order

वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य (तस्य) रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैः तत्प्रमाणैः महासुरैः सहस्रशः जगत् व्याप्तम् (आसीत्)।

vaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya (tasya) rudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥ tatpramāṇaiḥ mahāsuraiḥ sahasraśaḥ jagat vyāptam (āsīt) |

6Translation

LiteralBy great asuras of his size, born by thousands from the flow of blood of him who was split by Vaiṣṇavī’s discus, the world was filled.

ReadableFrom the blood that streamed from him when Vaiṣṇavī’s discus split him open, great asuras of his stature were born in thousands and filled the world.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe scale widens from the battlefield to the world (जगत्). The passive construction makes the asuras the agents and the world the thing overwhelmed.

8.49Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

शक्त्या जघान कौमारी वाराही च तथासिना।
माहेश्वरी त्रिशूलेन रक्तबीजं महासुरम् ॥ ४९ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

शक्त्या | जघान | कौमारी | वाराही | च | तथा | असिनामाहेश्वरी | त्रिशूलेन | रक्तबीजम् | महासुरम्

3IAST

As recitedśaktyā jaghāna kaumārī vārāhī ca tathāsinā |
māheśvarī triśūlena raktabījaṃ mahāsuram ||

Separatedśaktyā | jaghāna | kaumārī | vārāhī | ca | tathā | asinā
māheśvarī | triśūlena | raktabījam | mahāsuram

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
शक्त्याśaktyāwith the spearf. instr. sg. of शक्ति (the weapon, as in 8.34)
जघानjaghānastruck√हन् (adādi, 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; shared by the three subjects
कौमारीkaumārīKaumārīf. nom. sg.; subject

त्रिशूलेनtriśūlenawith the tridentn. instr. sg.; compound (bahuvrīhi, as in 8.34)
